In this work, we investigate the notion of LLM "biometrics" (analogous to human biometrics) to ask whether LLMs exhibit intrinsic fingerprints in weight space alone, without access to input data, that reveal their origin and lineage. We formulate this as a lineage discrimination problem, distinguishing among independent-origin, same-series, and shared-base models. To characterize these relationships, we propose a unified geometric fingerprinting framework that analyzes weight matrices from two complementary perspectives: (i) spectral energy, captured by singular value distributions to encode global magnitude patterns, and (ii) subspace alignment, quantified via subspace deviations to capture directional geometry. Our analysis uncovers a clear hierarchy of structural similarity in weight space: spectral energy reliably distinguishes independently trained models and different model families, while subspace alignment enables fine-grained discrimination among closely related models, including variations in dataset scale and post-training procedures. Extensive experiments on over 110 diverse open-weight LLM pairs demonstrate that weight-space geometry provides a robust and interpretable signal for model lineage, enabling coarse-grained regime separation and fine-grained discrimination within shared-base models.

</details>


### [83] [Evaluator Ensembles Under Reward Hacking: Covariance Geometry and Finite-Search Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08002)
*Fariya Afrin,Ibne Farabi Shihab*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 本文从协方差几何角度刻画了评估器集成中常见模式误差的不可辨识性，并给出有限搜索下的遗憾界与实用诊断工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前语言模型评估器或奖励模型在有限优化下可能被利用，导致响应质量未提升反而出现虚假高分。现有方法依赖评估器间分歧度诊断失败，但缺乏严格理论解释。

Method: 通过协方差几何分析评估器集成的公共模式误差与正交误差，证明公共模式误差不可从内部分数辨识；在联合次高斯模型下推导best-of-K与自适应搜索的遗憾界，提出有界双锚Bernstein证书及噪声代理的秩一协方差效应。

Result: 搜索项随log K平方根增长且高斯投影误差下渐近紧；固定种子高压测试与真实模型审计验证理论，并揭示高搜索压力下分歧度诊断的局限。

Conclusion: 分歧度无法可靠检测公共模式误差，需结合有界证书与误差模型进行稳健有限搜索评估。

Abstract: Language-model judges and reward models enable scalable supervision, but finite optimization can exploit evaluator errors rather than improve response quality. We characterize this failure through the covariance geometry of evaluator ensembles. For calibrated judges, the ensemble mean retains common-mode error along the all-ones direction, whereas cross-judge disagreement captures only orthogonal error. Consequently, disagreement can be high despite robust aggregation, or low while shared response-dependent errors persist. We prove that common-mode error is not identifiable from internal judge scores alone. Under a joint sub-Gaussian model, we bound best-of-K selection overstatement and target-quality regret, extending the guarantees to predictably adaptive search under conditional calibration. The resulting search terms scale as the square root of log K and are asymptotically tight for Gaussian projected errors. We further show that noisy quality proxies introduce artificial rank-one covariance without changing disagreement, and propose a bounded two-anchor Bernstein certificate for finite-search error and regret. Fixed-seed Gaussian stress tests over 120 (J, rho, K) configurations and real-model audits validate the theory while revealing the limits of disagreement-based diagnostics under increasing search pressure.

</details>


### [84] [Ground-Truth Neighborhood Regularization for Reinforcement Learning Post-Training of Time Series Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08010)
*Jianqi Zhang,Xingyu Zhang,Zeen Song,Changwen Zheng,Fanjiang Xu,Wenwen Qiang*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 本文针对时序基础模型在RL后训练中出现的输出分布偏离真实值的“次优坍缩”现象，提出基于真实值邻域正则化（GTN-R）的方法，通过引导模型概率质量靠近真实值邻域，提升采样质量与预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RL后训练可能使TSFMs的输出分布逐渐偏离真实值，限制下游任务性能；根本原因在于初始难以采样到靠近真实值的高质量轨迹。

Method: GTN-R以真实值为参考定位高质量区域，正则化模型概率质量向真实值邻域集中，增加高质量轨迹采样概率，可灵活集成于多种RL方法。

Result: 广泛实验验证了GTN-R在缓解次优坍缩和提升TSF性能上的有效性。

Conclusion: GTN-R有效缓解RL后训练中的次优坍缩，为TSFMs的RL微调提供稳健正则化手段，且具通用性。

Abstract: Time series forecasting (TSF) plays an important role in a wide range of real-world applications. Recently, time series foundation models (TSFMs), pretrained on large-scale datasets, have demonstrated strong generalization capabilities and emerged as an important paradigm for TSF. Reinforcement learning (RL) post-training has consequently attracted growing attention as a means of further improving their performance on downstream tasks. However, we find that, in certain forecast regions, RL post-training may gradually shift the output distributions of TSFMs away from the ground truth, thereby limiting their performance. We refer to this phenomenon as \textbf{suboptimal collapse}. Our analysis suggests that difficulty in initially sampling high-quality trajectories near the ground truth is an important contributing factor to suboptimal collapse. To address this issue, we propose Ground-Truth Neighborhood Regularization (GTN-R) for RL post-training of TSFMs. GTN-R uses the ground truth as a reference for locating high-quality regions and guides the model's probability mass toward the ground-truth neighborhood. This increases the probability of sampling high-quality trajectories, mitigates suboptimal collapse, and improves performance. Moreover, GTN-R can be flexibly integrated into various RL methods for TSFMs. Extensive experiments show its effectiveness.

</details>


### [85] [Support Selection Beyond Smooth DAG Exactness: Completion Geometry,Score Margins, and Selective Certificates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08103)
*Rui Wu,Zongyuan Chen,Hong Xie*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 研究DAG边界处光滑精确约束的退化性，揭示最小环补全生成单项式理想，并推导出选择时间的精确标度律，实验与理论一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分析仅针对特定约束公式的退化性，未隔离光滑精确性本身的影响，无法回答权重支撑是否为DAG及支撑应如何改变的问题。

Method: 从DAG边界出发分析最小环补全的代数结构，推导可行性时间与评分边界的标度律，并通过320条官方轨迹及有限样本置信族验证。

Result: 最小补全q边时首次响应阶数为q（向量残差）或2q（非负标量）；选择时间T0(ε)=Θ(ε^{-(2ν+1)})，ν=0时对数边界层；分离统计量预测时间（Spearman -0.52/-0.66，p<10^-4）；证书标签与oracle评分最优一致率100%，但与生成图不一致率分别为4.4%和5.5%。

Conclusion: 实验与理论一致，成功区分DAG可行性、基于评分的支撑选择和因果识别三类问题，并验证了退化性源于光滑精确性本身。

Abstract: Smooth acyclicity constraints answer whether a weighted support is a DAG, whereas structure learning asks which support change should be made. Existing analyses establish degeneracy for particular constraint formulas but do not isolate what follows from smooth exactness itself. At a DAG boundary, we show that minimal cycle completions generate a squarefree monomial ideal containing every restricted Taylor jet of an exact representation. If the smallest completion has $q$ edges, the first possible response has order $q$ for a vector residual and $2q$ for a nonnegative scalar. Exponentially many constant-scale cyclic manifolds exhibit the same lack of ranking away from the boundary for NOTEARS and DAGMA. We derive the exact selection time for an isolated cycle. When $Ψ'(h)\asymp h^ν$, the feasibility-only time is $T_0(\varepsilon)=Θ(\varepsilon^{-(2ν+1)})$; a score margin changes the leading dynamics at scale $T_0^{-1}$ for $ν>0$, while $ν=0$ has a logarithmic boundary layer requiring $γT_0\log(1/\varepsilon)\to0$. Experiments verify this law, and a truth-free separation statistic predicts selection time on 320 official NOTEARS/DAGMA trajectories (Spearman $-0.52$ and $-0.66$, permutation $p<10^{-4}$). For finite samples, a parent-set confidence family and forced-opposite queries certify skeleton and unshielded-collider labels shared by every population optimum of a frozen score. Across 320 runs, every regret bound covers an independent oracle-score audit. None of 3,042 certified skeleton or 2,396 collider labels disagrees with the oracle-score optimum, although 4.4% and 5.5%, respectively, disagree with the generating graph. These results separate DAG feasibility, score-based support selection, and causal identification.

</details>


### [86] [TSDS-Toolbox: A Toolbox for Measuring Time-Series Dataset Similarity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08119)
*Yen-Ku Liu,Hongjie Chen,Ryan A. Rossi,Franck Dernoncourt*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 提出了一个统一的时间序列数据集相似性工具箱(TSDS-Toolbox),用于系统化、可复现地比较时间序列数据集相似性方法,支持灵活扩展和一致评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列数据集相似性方法的基准实现分散且难以扩展,阻碍了在基础模型微调等场景中的源数据集选择研究。

Method: 构建统一框架,支持数据集级和序列级相似性方法的集成比较,提供数据集缩减器以实现一致评估,并支持用户自定义数据集、相似性方法和下游任务。

Result: 通过多样化实验设置下的全面实验验证了工具箱的有效性,并公开提供工具箱。

Conclusion: TSDS-Toolbox为时间序列数据集相似性研究提供了标准化、可扩展的基准平台,有助于推动该领域的发展。

Abstract: The rapid advancement of artificial intelligence (AI) has significantly accelerated research in time-series analysis, particularly in forecasting, classification, and generation tasks. Recent models, especially foundation models, benefit from time-series dataset similarity due to its significant role in source dataset selection for fine-tuning. However, many existing implementations for benchmarking time-series dataset similarity methods are fragmented and difficult to extend. To address this, we present a unified framework, the Time-Series Dataset Similarity Toolbox (TSDS-Toolbox). Our work enables (1) systematic and reproducible comparisons of time-series dataset similarity methods; (2) flexible extensibility for users to add customized datasets, similarity methods, and downstream time-series tasks; and (3) consistent evaluation of both dataset-level and series-level similarity methods through integrated time-series dataset reducers. The effectiveness of TSDS-Toolbox is validated through comprehensive experiments under diverse experimental settings. Our toolbox is publicly available.

</details>


### [87] [When Is Benchmark Contamination Detectable? Information Limits and Power-Calibrated Audits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07914)
*Ibne Farabi Shihab,Sanjeda Akter,Anuj Sharma*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 本文形式化区分了行为污染检测中“无证据”是因基准干净还是审计功效不足，提出以功效、预算和有效性三重门控来解读非拒绝结果，并发现基于功效的高斯预算在小样本下失准，但预声明的两阶段规划器可修复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 行为污染检测器返回“无证据”时无法区分基准确实干净或审计功效过低，导致结论不可解释，需要形式化这一区别并建立可验证的审计契约。

Method: 通过匹配干净与已见对照，将行为通道建模为稀疏混合分布，利用二阶矩论证推导可检测性由 alpha * rho * sqrt(m) 控制；定义标量检测器功效上界为 rho，并引入样本分割证书无分布地下界 alpha；提出预声明两阶段规划器，通过模拟部署测试修复预算失准问题。

Result: 冻结校准功效可预测留出功效曲线（R^2=0.83-0.98），但功效-only高斯预算在9/9门控通道中失准；两阶段规划器保守且可弃权；证书有效但审计规模下空洞；五种子配对注入研究揭示机制排序为逐字>释义>表面，答案-only信号由基线漂移解释。

Conclusion: 非拒绝结果必须结合功效、预算和有效性门控一同解读，审计契约应同时报告成功与失败，以确保可解释性和可靠性。

Abstract: Behavioral contamination detectors can return "no evidence" either because a benchmark is clean or because the audit has little power. We formalize this distinction for a benchmark in which an unknown fraction alpha of items was seen during training. With matched clean and seen controls, the behavioral channel is the sparse mixture Q_alpha = (1 - alpha) P_0 + alpha P_1, and an exact second-moment argument shows that detectability is governed by alpha * rho * sqrt(m), where rho^2 = chi^2(P_1 || P_0) measures behavioral separability. Any scalar detector reduces to its efficacy, ef = |E_1 f - E_0 f| / sqrt(Var_0(f)) <= rho, which can be estimated from controls before the audit is run. A separate sample-split certificate lower-bounds alpha distribution-free, without requiring an orientation assumption. Our empirical finding is two-sided. Frozen calibration efficacy predicts held-out power curves, with R^2 = 0.83-0.98 across six exact-permutation channels, but the efficacy-only Gaussian budget is miscalibrated at the small sample sizes it prescribes, failing in 9/9 gate-passing channels even though efficacy itself transports. The failure is in the inversion, not the calibration. A predeclared two-stage planner that simulates the deployed test repairs the budgets, is uniformly conservative, and abstains when its probe does not transport. The certificate is valid but vacuous at audit scale, and a five-seed paired injection study recovers the mechanism ordering verbatim > paraphrase > surface, in which the apparent answer-only signal is explained by baseline drift. We report the audit contract and its failures together: a non-rejection is interpretable only alongside the efficacy, budget, and validity gates that produced it.

</details>


### [88] [Beyond Aggregate Calibration: Decomposing Income-Conditional Recall Disparities in Automated Credit Default Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08202)
*Sai Srikar Boddupalli*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 研究发现，数据中心的噪声过滤流程在大型消费贷款数据集中存在人口统计偏差，高收入违约者更易被标注为标签噪声，且在剔除敏感属性后偏差仍通过结构代理变量存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估基于模型置信度的数据清理管线在金融场景中的公平性影响，揭示其对不同收入群体的差异化后果。

Method: 采用LendingClub数据集（N=1,344,936），结合等机会度量、序列特征遮蔽方法以及SHAP值分析，隔离收入、利率和残余偏差的贡献。

Result: 高收入违约者的召回率低于低收入者16.86个百分点；剔除收入和利率后残余偏差仍达2.56-3.55个百分点（Z=-4.04, p<0.0001），主要由贷款金额和住房所有权驱动。

Conclusion: 仅屏蔽敏感属性无法确保公平，机构定价和代理变量会重建被隐藏的信号；建议在受监管金融机构中采用更全面的数据审计策略。

Abstract: Data-centric curation pipelines frequently rely on model confidence scores to flag and filter noisy or mislabeled training instances. Evaluating this filtering convention on a large-scale consumer lending sample (LendingClub, N = 1,344,936) uncovers an underlying demographic asymmetry: high-income defaulters are disproportionately classified as label noise relative to low-income defaulters (Cramer's V approximately 0.03-0.07). Re-examining this behavior through the lens of equal opportunity [Hardt et al., 2016] reveals a far more severe discrepancy: a 16.86 percentage point gap in true positive rate (recall) between high- and low-income borrowers who ultimately defaulted. Implementing a sequential feature-blinding methodology allows us to isolate the drivers of this disparity across three distinct mechanisms: (1) direct reliance on self-reported applicant income; (2) algorithmic absorption of upstream institutional bias encoded within origination interest rates; and (3) a residual disparity (3.55 percentage points in cross-validation; 2.56 percentage points on a held-out test partition, Z = -4.04, p < 0.0001) that remains even after purging both income and interest rates from the model. Out-of-sample signed SHAP valuations demonstrate that this residual gap is maintained by structural proxies, most notably loan amount and home ownership status. These empirical findings show that simply blinding an algorithm to sensitive attributes fails to ensure fairness when institutional pricing decisions and behavioral proxy variables collectively reconstruct the omitted signals. We outline the practical implications of these findings for auditing data-centric AI workflows within regulated financial institutions.

</details>


### [89] [Guixu: Valuation-Driven Data Discovery for Autonomous AI Agents with On-Chain Attestation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07949)
*Yifan Wu,Yuchen Peng,Jiaqi Chai,Yufei Qian,Xilin Li,Ke Chen,Lidan Shou*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 本文提出Guixu，一个面向自主代理的、基于价值驱动的数据发现系统，通过三阶段估值流程、多轮背包优化和链上数据市场，实现任务感知、预算受限且可信的数据发现与采购。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据发现系统仅面向检索，缺乏任务效用估计、预算约束下的成本效益选择及可信反馈整合，无法满足自主代理对数据下游任务的支持需求。

Method: Guixu采用带代理标签传播的三阶段估值流程，结合多轮背包优化进行任务感知的数据估值，集成代理支付协议支持预算受限的采购工作流，并利用链上数据市场和证明信号实现可验证的数据发现。

Result: 演示表明，Guixu使代理能够超越基于关键词的数据集检索，实现任务和预算感知的可信数据发现与采购，支持从自然语言任务指定、多源搜索到数据估值和可验证交易反馈的完整工作流。

Conclusion: Guixu为自主代理提供了一种替代传统检索式数据发现的新范式，通过估值驱动和可验证机制提升数据采购的效用与可信度。

Abstract: Autonomous agents increasingly rely on external data to complete downstream tasks such as model training and decision support. However, existing data discovery systems remain largely retrieval-oriented: they surface candidate datasets from heterogeneous sources, but provide limited support for estimating task-specific utility, selecting cost-effective datasets under budget constraints, or incorporating trustworthy feedback from prior usage. This paper presents Guixu, a valuation-driven data discovery system for autonomous agents. Guixu employs a three-phase valuation pipeline with proxy-label propagation and multi-round knapsack optimization for task-aware data valuation. Guixu integrates agentic payment protocol to enable budget-constrained data procurement workflows. Guixu leverages on-chain data market and attestation signals for verifiable data discovery. Our demonstration highlights how Guixu enables an agent to move beyond keyword-based dataset retrieval toward task- and budget-aware, trustworthy data discovery and procurement. Attendees can interactively explore the full workflow, from NL task specification and multi-source search to data valuation and verifiable transaction feedback.

</details>


### [90] [Learning from Environmental Feedback: Credit Assignment across Multiple Timescales for Agentic Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08255)
*Yifu Huo,Shunjie Xing,Chenglong Wang,Peinan Feng,Qiaozhi He,Yan Ding,Anxiang Ma,Yuxin Gao,Tongran Liu,Tong Xiao,Jingbo Zhu*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 提出EFCA，一种基于环境反馈的多时间尺度信用分配方法，通过短期反馈和中期状态历史信号改善长程智能体强化学习的稀疏奖励问题，在ALFWorld和WebShop上提升任务成功率与质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有信用分配方法忽视了环境交互中自然生成的过程信息（如交互历史），而这些信息能为单个动作的贡献提供宝贵监督，有助于解决智能体RL中延迟和稀疏奖励的挑战。

Method: EFCA使用长期结果信号补充两种环境相关过程信号：短期反馈信号（捕捉当前动作的即时效果）和中期状态历史信号（识别近期交互中的低效模式），并通过回报重加权机制整合这些信号。

Result: 在ALFWorld和WebShop上的实验表明，EFCA consistently优于强基线，同时提高任务成功率和任务质量。

Conclusion: 环境基础的多时间尺度信用分配对长程智能体RL有效，验证了利用环境反馈过程信息提升信用分配性能的可行性。

Abstract: Agentic reinforcement learning (RL) often suffers from delayed and sparse rewards in real-world environments. A promising solution to this challenge is credit assignment, which aims to decompose trajectory-level rewards and provide more fine-grained supervision for intermediate decisions. However, existing credit assignment approaches ignore the rich process information naturally generated during environment interaction, e.g., interaction history. We argue that such information provides valuable supervision for identifying the contribution of individual actions. To this end, we propose Environmental Feedback-based Credit Assignment (EFCA), a multi-timescale credit assignment approach for long-horizon agentic RL. EFCA complements the long-term outcome signal with two environment-grounded process signals: a short-term feedback signal that captures the immediate effect of the current action and a medium-term state-history signal that identifies ineffective patterns from recent interactions. Both signals are directly extracted from environment feedback and integrated through a return reweighting mechanism. Experiments on ALFWorld and WebShop demonstrate that EFCA consistently improves both task success and task quality over strong baselines, highlighting the effectiveness of environment-grounded multi-timescale credit assignment for long-horizon agentic RL.

</details>


### [91] [A Controlled Study of Feature-Based Knowledge Distillation Across Student Designs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08294)
*Abhinand Balachandran,Praveen Prashant*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 该研究系统比较了知识蒸馏中基于特征的方法（Attention Transfer、FitNets）与标准logit蒸馏在不同规模学生网络上的效果差异，发现特征约束并非普遍有益，且固定辅助系数会导致梯度尺度不一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 特征蒸馏在训练学生网络时额外对齐中间表示，但这种额外约束可能对不同学生产生不同影响，此前缺乏系统性的跨学生规模与结构的对比研究。

Method: 在CIFAR-100上使用ResNet-50教师，分别以不同宽度的CustomResNet家族和MobileNetV2为学生，对每个特征方法与匹配的logit-KD基线进行对照实验，严格控制教师、优化器、训练计划、随机种子等变量，并多次重复验证。

Result: Logit蒸馏稳定提升所有学生；Attention Transfer在CustomResNet中平均为负效果且与宽度无明确关联，但在MobileNetV2上为正；FitNets在所有15组配对实验中均低于logit蒸馏，且在同一深度下宽度越大性能差距越大，但不同深度的w=48学生未遵循此趋势；固定辅助系数导致不同学生的梯度尺度差异显著。

Conclusion: 特征蒸馏方法的有效性高度依赖学生架构与规模，不存在统一的最优选择；使用固定辅助系数无法为不同学生提供等价训练条件，未来需针对学生特性调整蒸馏策略。

Abstract: Knowledge distillation trains a smaller student to match the outputs of a larger teacher. Feature-based methods also align intermediate representations, but this extra constraint may affect students differently. We study this question on CIFAR-100 using a ResNet-50 teacher, a width-controlled CustomResNet family and MobileNetV2 as a cross-design comparison. For each student, we evaluate each feature method against a matched logit-KD run using the same teacher, optimizer settings, training schedule and seed. We repeat the main comparisons across multiple seeds.
  Logit KD improved every tested student over its scratch baseline. Attention Transfer showed no clear relationship with size inside the CustomResNet family, but its average effect was negative for that family and positive for MobileNetV2. FitNets was below logit KD in all 15 paired runs. Within the constant-depth width sweep, its gap increased for wider students, although the different-depth w=48 student did not follow this trend. Finally, the same auxiliary coefficient produced different gradient scales across students, showing that a fixed coefficient does not create a uniform training condition.

</details>


### [92] [The Authority Expectancy Effect in Multi-User Conflict](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08026)
*Eunna Lee*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 本研究系统探究了社会权威信号与大语言模型严重性优先级之间的交互作用，提出了权威期望效应（AEE），并揭示了其参照依赖、证据重释和方向敏感三大特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究通常将权威信号视为可加权的独立因素，但未考虑其与严重性优先级之间的复杂交互，可能导致对模型判断机制的误解。

Method: 通过四个LLM（Claude、Gemini、GPT、Grok）和三个实验阶段（资源分配、故障归因、多轮争议调解），将社会权威和严重性优先级分别操作化为模型引发的基线，并系统比较权威信号与证据对齐/不对齐时的判断变化。

Result: 发现职业权威、制度文档和关系一致性会以非加性方式重构模型判断，AEE表现出参照依赖、证据重释和方向敏感三种可复现的性质。

Conclusion: 权威信号并非简单叠加于严重性优先级之上，而是通过复杂的认知机制影响模型推理，需在AI对齐与评估中纳入此类交互效应。

Abstract: We investigate how social authority (SA) signals interact with severity-based prioritization in large language models, operationalizing each axis as a model-elicited baseline -- the triage hierarchy and the SA hierarchy. Across four LLMs (Claude, Gemini, GPT, Grok) and three experimental phases -- resource allocation, fault attribution, and multi-turn dispute mediation -- we find that occupational authority, institutional documentation, and relational congruence can restructure model judgments in ways not captured by additive reweighting of authority cues. We formalize this pattern as the Authority Expectancy Effect (AEE) and characterize it through three properties observed across our conditions: it is reference-dependent, defined only relative to a pre-authority baseline; it involves evidential reinterpretation, in which identical content acquires different inferential implications depending on which party bears the SA signal; and it exhibits direction sensitivity, producing opposite outcomes depending on whether authority position and evidentiary cues align.

</details>


### [93] [Unimodality-Promoting Regularized Learning for Ordinal Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08359)
*Ryoya Yamasaki*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种更严格遵循单峰性正则化思想的序数回归方法，避免以往方法引入的尺度偏差，并通过实验验证其在提升预测性能上的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究表明序数数据的目标变量条件概率分布通常具有单峰性，但现有UPRL方法在促进单峰性的同时会引入尺度偏差（使预测分布更平滑、置信度降低），从而损害预测性能，尤其在训练数据较小时。

Method: 通过分析现有UPRL方法的尺度偏差来源，设计一种新的正则化策略，严格促进预测条件概率分布的单峰性而不扩大其尺度（即保持置信度），并基于此构建新的序数回归学习方法。

Result: 实验比较显示，所提方法在小规模数据或较大训练数据下优于现有UPRL方法，且验证了单峰性促进本身确实有助于提升预测性能；同时分析揭示了性能差异与是否引入尺度偏差相关。

Conclusion: 严格避免尺度偏差的单峰性正则化能更有效地利用序数数据的固有结构，提升序数回归的预测精度，尤其在小样本场景下具有优势。

Abstract: Ordinal regression, also called ordinal classification, is classification of ordinal data, in which the underlying target variable is categorical and considered to have a natural ordinal relation. Previous works have indicated that, in many real-world ordinal data, the conditional probability distribution (CPD) of the target variable given a value of the explanatory variable would be unimodal in a large domain of the explanatory variable and close to be unimodal even in a remaining domain. Therefore, unimodality-promoting regularized learning (UPRL), which promotes a predicted CPD closer to be unimodal with the aim of decreasing a prediction variance without inducing much bias for ordinal data of the unimodality, is promising to improve the prediction performance especially with small-size training data. In this study, we show that previous UPRL methods promote a predicted CPD to not only become closer to be unimodal but also have a larger scale (in other words, be smoother or less-confident). Therefore, we develop a novel method that more strictly reflects the idea of UPRL and evades a scale-related bias, and verify through experimental comparison that the unimodality-promotion indeed contributes to improve the prediction performance. Additionally, while our proposed UPRL method could perform better for smaller-scale data or with larger-size training data compared to a previous UPRL method, our analysis explains this experimental observation in terms of the presence or absence of an unexpected scale-related bias.

</details>


### [94] [H2: A Dual Hybrid Semantic Data Lake Architecture for Medical Data Harmonization with Human-In-the-Loop verified, LLM Driven Metadata Annotation System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08056)
*Ioannis N. Tzortzis,Georgia Kapetadimitri,Agapi Davradou,Nefeli Kousta,Nikolaos Bakalos,Ioannis Rallis,Dimitrios Kalogeras,Nikolaos Doulamis,Anastasios Doulamis*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 提出一种基于知识图谱和LLM的语义数据湖架构，通过自动生成元数据注释，实现医学异构数据的协调与机器学习操作的适配性评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学数据存在多模态、多模式及非结构化等异构问题，数据湖虽能统一存储但易陷入‘数据沼泽’，且数据互操作性差，ML方法选择依赖专家手动判断，缺乏自动化支持。

Method: 设计语义数据湖架构，利用知识图谱动态描述数据关系，避免预定义模式；引入LLM对未标注元数据进行生成式注释，自动标记适用ML操作，进而构建高层知识以评估数据与ML任务的适配性。

Result: 通过生成式注释与知识图谱结合，提升了数据协调能力，并为无标签数据集提供了ML适用性推断，减少了人工干预并扩展了数据使用价值。

Conclusion: 该架构有效缓解医学数据异构与‘数据沼泽’问题，为自动化ML任务选型提供可靠基础，未来可进一步优化注释生成质量与扩展多场景应用。

Abstract: Medical data, by its nature, exhibit a high degree of heterogeneity on multiple levels ranging from (a) different modalities like images, text and time series, (b) diverse tabular schemata introduced by institutions and (c) completely unstructured textual information data provided by healthcare professionals. Data lakes are often used in medical data storage to consolidate all heterogeneous diverse data in a single, central location, where it can be saved "as is", without the need to impose a schema like a data warehouse does. Despite their flexibility, though, data lakes are notorious for the "data swamp" failure. Thus, providing a reliable data harmonization mechanism through metadata, without compromising integrity or flexibility, is a real challenge. To this end, knowledge graphs have attracted attention since they provide a dynamic way to depict relationships without a rigid schema-on-write approach. Additionally, another rigorous task relies on the interoperability of data: application of appropriate ML techniques on such a diverse nature of data is not an easy task, as a domain expert must decide the efficacy of a method to a specific data type or dataset. Metadata annotation can aid by tagging applicable operations, however this requires manual intervention, not to mention the plethora of existing datasets which lack such information. To tackle both challenges, in this paper, we propose a semantic data lake architecture that promotes data harmonization and incorporates a generative annotation process (i.e. LLMs) of non-labeled metadata collections to support the application of meaningful ML techniques. Building on top of this approach, we create a higher level of knowledge, identifying suitability of data with respect to applicable ML operations based on their data nature...

</details>


### [95] [Exact Rank and Convex Calibration Dimension Lower Bounds for the Multi-Label F1 Loss](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08399)
*Mingyuan Zhang*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 本文确定了多标签分类中实例级F1度量的损失矩阵的精确秩，并证明其凸校准代理的最小维度为Θ(s²)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: F1度量是多标签分类的核心性能指标，此前已知其存在二次维度的凸校准替代函数，但精确的秩和凸校准维度的下界未知，本文填补了这一空白。

Method: 通过将非空得分矩阵分解为子集关联矩阵和正定柯西矩阵的乘积来确定精确秩；通过构造特定分布并利用可行子空间下界来分析Bayes几何，推导凸校准维度的下界。

Result: F1得分矩阵、平移损失矩阵和未平移损失矩阵的秩均为s²-s+2，列仿射维度为s²-s+1；凸校准维度满足CCdim(L^{F1})≥(2/(3√3)-o(1))s²，结合上界得到CCdim=Θ(s²)。

Conclusion: F1度量的凸校准代理所需的最小维度与标签数的平方同阶，确认了其内在复杂度为二次增长。

Abstract: The instance-wise $F_1$ measure is a central performance measure for multi-label classification. For a problem with $s$ labels, it defines a $2^s\times 2^s$ loss matrix. Previous work exhibited $s^2+1$-coordinate affine and shifted low-rank representations and used them to construct quadratic-dimensional convex calibrated surrogates. We determine the exact rank. Under the convention $F_1(\varnothing,\varnothing)=1$, the $F_1$ score matrix, the shifted loss matrix, and the unshifted loss matrix all have rank $s^2-s+2$, while the column-affine dimension of the loss is $s^2-s+1$. The proof factors the nonempty score matrix through subset-incidence matrices and a positive-definite Cauchy matrix.
  Exact rank does not, by itself, lower-bound the dimension of an arbitrary convex calibrated surrogate. We therefore analyze the Bayes geometry of $F_1$ directly. We construct a distribution for which precisely all supersets of a fixed core label set are Bayes optimal, and show that the corresponding active loss columns, restricted to the witness support, have affine dimension $hn$, where $n=s-\lfloor s/3\rfloor$ and $h=\lceil(s\lfloor s/3\rfloor)^{1/2}\rceil-1$. Applying the feasible-subspace lower bound for convex calibration dimension gives \[
  \operatorname{CCdim}(L^{F_1})
  \ge \left(\frac{2}{3\sqrt{3}}-o(1)\right)s^2. \] Together with the quadratic upper bound, this establishes $\operatorname{CCdim}(L^{F_1})=Θ(s^2)$.

</details>


### [96] [PATH: Next-Interval Prediction via Autoregressive Tree Hierarchy on Tabular Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08078)
*Pengxiang Cai,Wanchen Lian,Chenyang Liu,Xiaohan Li,Qingyuan Zeng,Jinhong Wang,Jintai Chen*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 提出PATH方法，通过将预测区间建模为嵌套子区间的层次结构，并利用自回归解码器学习概率流，在56个OpenML数据集上将区间长度降至0.1473，同时保持覆盖率达0.9144，优于24种基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有区间预测方法通常先预测不确定性代理，再通过校准或选择转换为区间，这导致后处理规则决定最终区间，未能充分利用学习到的输出分布信息，从而产生较长的区间。

Method: 将预测区间构建为递归嵌套结构，用二叉树表示；将问题形式化为下一区间预测，学习每个区间到其子区间的概率流；使用基础叶分布加上自回归解码器细化分支概率，最终通过累积相邻区间概率并选择达到目标质量的最短连续范围来输出预测区间。

Result: 在PATHBench（56个OpenML回归数据集）上与24种基线比较，PATH取得最低平均归一化长度0.1473，同时保持平均覆盖率0.9144，显著缩短了预测区间。

Conclusion: 层次化输出建模是表格数据上实现紧凑区间预测的有效方法，代码已公开。

Abstract: Interval prediction aims to achieve a target coverage level while producing intervals that are as short as possible. Many conformal regression pipelines first predict an uncertainty surrogate and then convert it into an interval through calibration or selection. This separation supports coverage calibration, but post hoc rules largely determine the final interval and do not fully use the learned output distribution. We observe that the resulting intervals have inherently hierarchical geometry: an interval can be recursively refined into nested subintervals, and binary trees naturally represent this structure. We formulate this hierarchy as next-interval prediction and propose PATH, which learns how probability mass flows from each interval to its next nested subintervals. PATH predicts a base leaf distribution and uses an autoregressive decoder to refine branch probabilities. Matching the distribution to the interval hierarchy aligns learning with extraction: PATH accumulates probability over adjacent output intervals and returns the shortest contiguous range reaching a selected mass. We compare PATH with 24 baselines for interval prediction on PATHBench, comprising 56 OpenML regression datasets. PATH substantially shortens the resulting intervals, achieving the lowest mean normalized length, 0.1473, while maintaining mean coverage of 0.9144. These results establish hierarchical output modeling as an effective approach for compact interval prediction on tabular data. Code is publicly available at https://github.com/pxcai/PATH.

</details>


### [97] [Constraining ontology mappings using metaphysical choices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08122)
*Giacomo De Colle,Helena Blackmore,Chris Partridge*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 提出一种基于本体论形而上学承诺的语义映射验证方法，并以IES与BFO间的映射为例，通过SPARQL查询实现基数约束验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语义映射验证缺乏对本体论根基的考量，导致跨本体映射的可靠性和一致性不足。

Method: 构建以本体形而上学承诺为基础的验证框架，针对IES与BFO映射定义基数约束，并利用SPARQL查询操作化验证流程。

Result: 成功展示了该方法在映射管道结果验证中的可行性，具体通过SPARQL查询实现了基数约束检查。

Conclusion: 该验证框架为跨本体映射提供了理论驱动的实用验证途径，未来可推广至其他本体组合。

Abstract: In this paper we discuss the foundations behind a novel methodology for the validation of semantic mappings between different data sources based upon different foundation ontologies, where the methodology builds a framework based upon the metaphysical commitments of the ontologies. We provide as example the test case of mappings between IES and BFO, and we especially focused on providing cardinality constraints on the mappings between the two ontologies. In order to demonstrate the applicability of our method, we showcased how these principles can be operationalized through SPARQL queries validating the results of a mapping pipeline.

</details>


### [98] [Out-of-Distribution Federated Distillation with Domain-Aware Proxy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08525)
*Jiahao Xiao,Jiangming Liu*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 提出一种面向域感知的代理数据选择框架，提升联邦蒸馏在分布外场景下的泛化性能，在标准基准上平均准确率达82.9%和80.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联邦蒸馏方法在分布内数据上表现良好，但难以适应分布外场景，代理数据的选择缺乏域感知能力，导致模型泛化性不足。

Method: 设计域感知代理选择框架，根据目标域与代理域的相似性动态筛选或加权代理数据，以更有效地适配OOD分布偏移。

Result: 在有无代理数据两种OOD设置下，相比现有方法平均准确率分别提升82.9%和80.6%。

Conclusion: 所提框架能显著改善联邦蒸馏在分布外场景下的表现，代码和数据集已公开以供复现。

Abstract: Federated Learning is a distributed machine learning paradigm that trains a global model by aggregating local clients without sharing private data of each client. Federated Distillation (FD) builds upon this paradigm by leveraging knowledge distillation to exchange soft predictions on proxy data instead of model parameters, enabling more efficient communication and supporting heterogeneous model collaboration. However, FD models trained on In-Distribution data are hardly adapted to Out-of-Distribution (OOD) scenarios. In this paper, we propose a domain-aware proxy selection framework to better adopt proxy data for OOD problems. The experimental results show that the proposed models effectively address the challenges of distribution shifts under OOD with and without proxy data by achieving average 82.9\% and 80.6\% over existing works on standard benchmarks. The codes and data are released in https://anonymous.4open.science/r/DPS-FD-8596.

</details>


### [99] [Improving Constraint Models with LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08127)
*Florentina Voboril,Stefan Szeider*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 提出一个智能体框架，利用LLM自动重构约束规划模型，通过经验验证而非构造保证正确性，在多数实例上显著提升求解效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 约束规划求解器性能对建模选择高度敏感，传统改进依赖专家经验，现有自动重构系统仅限于预定义规则库。

Method: LLM智能体基于给定模型和三个训练实例，生成候选变体，通过将解注入原模型验证正确性，并迭代诊断修复，最终在更大测试实例上评估。

Result: 在9个组合优化问题的27个测试实例中，21个优于原始模型，部分问题求解速度提升两个数量级以上，且优于非智能体基线。

Conclusion: 自主智能体方法能有效支持约束模型改进，诊断与修复过程是关键优势。

Abstract: The runtime of Constraint Programming (CP) solvers is highly sensitive to modeling choices, such as symmetry breaking, implied constraints, global constraints, constraint reformulation, and variable representation. Improving these constraint models has traditionally required human expertise, and existing automated reformulation systems are restricted to a predefined library of hand-crafted transformation rules. We introduce an agentic framework that instead reformulates a constraint model from an open-ended space and establishes correctness empirically rather than by construction: a Large Language Model (LLM) agent, given a model and three training instances, proposes alternative formulations, validates each by injecting its solution back into the original model, and diagnoses and repairs failures, returning the best variant it finds in a median of about fifteen minutes. The models are expressed in the CPMpy modeling library, and each proposed model is evaluated on three larger test instances. Across nine combinatorial optimization problems, the generated models outperform the originals on 21 of 27 test instances, and on some problems solve more than two orders of magnitude faster. A comparison against non-agentic baselines that reuse the same validation and selection tools indicates that the gains stem from the agent's iterative diagnosis and repair, not merely from sampling several candidates. These results demonstrate that autonomous agentic methods can support the improvement of constraint models.

</details>


### [100] [TokenPrint: A Calibrated Token-Space Fingerprint for Language-Model Provenance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08139)
*Yuqi Wu,Shengming Zhao,Jie Chen*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 提出一种基于知识探针和顶层隐藏状态词汇投影的免训练指纹方法，用于语言模型溯源与血缘检索，在32个开源模型上验证了有效性、深度规律和量化稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型的溯源（基座检查点、训练分布重叠）是治理难题，元数据不足以解决，需要新的技术手段。

Method: 提取250个固定知识探针引发的晚期隐藏状态，取top-k词汇投影，用Jaccard重叠比较解码后的token字符串，形成训练无关指纹。

Result: 相似度阶梯与模型关系一致（独立同数据0.48，共享基座微调0.39，同开发者0.38，无关0.22）；血缘检索对5个R1蒸馏模型平均排名1.8（MRR 0.60）；深度上AUC从0.72（1/4深度）升至0.90（输出层）；int8下Jaccard 0.92，int4下0.82-0.85。

Conclusion: 该指纹在模型血缘识别上有效、鲁棒且免训练，为模型溯源治理提供了可行工具。

Abstract: Establishing the provenance of a language model---including its base checkpoint and possible overlap in training distributions---is a governance challenge that metadata alone cannot resolve. We introduce a training-free fingerprint based on the top-$k$ vocabulary projections of late hidden states elicited by 250 fixed knowledge probes, compared using Jaccard overlap over decoded token strings. We evaluate the method on 32 open-weight models from nine families (0.6B--32B) with documented relationships. (1)~A \emph{similarity ladder} broadly follows model relatedness: independently trained models on identical data score 0.48 raw (0.35 vocabulary-corrected), followed by shared-base fine-tunes (0.39/0.33), same-developer relatives (0.38/0.28), and models with no documented relationship (0.22/0.17). This identical-data signal persists across three organizations, two tokenizer families, and two architecture classes, and emerges within the first 1\% of training before measurable task competence, suggesting a contribution from shared training data beyond capability convergence. (2)~As a nearest-neighbor \emph{lineage-retrieval} method, the fingerprint ranks the exact documented base among the top two candidates for all five R1 distillations (mean rank 1.8, MRR 0.60), including a math-specialized base not identifiable from coarse metadata. (3)~A \emph{depth ablation} shows that lineage group discrimination strengthens toward the output distribution, with AUC increasing from 0.72 at quarter depth to 0.90 at the output; using only the top 5 output tokens retains AUC 0.87. (4)~The fingerprint remains stable under quantization, with Jaccard similarity of 0.92 under int8 and 0.82--0.85 under int4, compared with a maximum cross-model similarity of 0.81 in the calibration pool. We release the probes, code, and fingerprints.

</details>


### [101] [When Can Fraud Operations Authorize Automation? A Decision-Support Framework for Fresh Audit Evidence and Review Workload](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08577)
*Jie Deng*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 提出一种考虑证据新鲜度与审核容量的欺诈决策框架，在选择性延迟标签下安全自动化审批，并在三个数据集上验证了其效果与权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 欺诈运营需在自动批准、人工审核和自动拦截之间分配事件，但用于评估这些行为的标签具有选择性和延迟性。预测分数虽能排序案件，却无法反映证据是否足够新颖和具有代表性以将决策授权给模型。

Method: 开发了 freshness-constrained audit capacity (FCAC) 框架，将自动化视为受行动风险、证据新鲜度和共享审核容量约束的授权决策。基于成熟的随机审计和预设的时间容限评估候选行动区域，支持的区域自动化，不支持的保留人工审核，并输出证据年龄、审计需求、总审核工作量、价值暴露及兼容的时间变化等决策记录。

Result: 在 IEEE-CIS、ULB-Worldline 和 Elliptic++ 上，零漂移自动化率分别为 84.4%、67.4% 和 81.3%，总审核工作量分别为 24.1%、46.0% 和 43.1%。实验揭示了审计容量权衡：稀疏审计延迟授权，密集审计增加工作量。BAF 压力测试表明回退阈值应基于候选特定证据而非风险限制的共同比例。

Conclusion: 审计新鲜度和分析师容量是欺诈决策支持中的联合设计考量，对安全自动化和工作量平衡具有重要意义。

Abstract: Fraud operations must allocate events among automatic approval, analyst review, and automatic blocking even though the labels needed to evaluate these actions are selective and delayed. Predictive scores order cases, but they do not show whether the evidence is current and representative enough to delegate an action to the model. We develop freshness-constrained audit capacity (FCAC), a decision-support framework that treats automation as an authorization decision constrained by action risk, evidence freshness, and shared review capacity. It evaluates candidate action regions from mature randomized audits and a prespecified temporal allowance. Supported regions are automated; unsupported regions remain in review. The resulting decision record reports evidence age, audit demand, total review workload, value exposure, and compatible temporal change. We show that current action risk is unidentified without restricting unobserved label evolution. Under representative randomized audits, label-independent evidence windows, and a prespecified condition linking historical and current action risk, we derive simultaneous finite-sample control of unsafe authorization. Chronological evaluations with simulated audits on IEEE-CIS, ULB-Worldline, and Elliptic++ yield zero-drift automation rates of 84.4%, 67.4%, and 81.3%, with total review workloads of 24.1%, 46.0%, and 43.1%. The experiments reveal an audit-capacity trade-off: sparse auditing delays authorization, whereas intensive auditing eventually increases workload. A separately specified BAF stress test further indicates that fallback thresholds must reflect candidate-specific evidence rather than a common fraction of the risk limit. These findings identify audit freshness and analyst capacity as joint design considerations for fraud decision support.

</details>


### [102] [Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning via Agent-Specific Preference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08604)
*Ni Mu,Yao Luan,Yiqin Yang,Qing-Shan Jia*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 提出MAGPIE框架，用代理特定偏好建模替代全局奖励设计，通过分散偏好优化收敛至纳什均衡，并实验验证其在多智能体任务中的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体强化学习依赖精心设计的全局奖励，但在异构智能体系统中，单一标量目标难以捕捉多样行为，奖励工程成本高且难以实施。

Method: 为每个智能体配备独立专家提供偏好信号，构建代理特定奖励模型，并通过单调聚合机制整合为全局目标；理论证明该聚合优化等价于纳什均衡策略训练。

Result: 在基准多智能体任务和顺序生产线任务中，MAGPIE性能与精心工程化奖励的基线相当，且无需精确奖励设计。

Conclusion: MAGPIE在奖励工程不可行场景下能有效促进策略学习，提供了一种替代全局奖励设计的可行方案。

Abstract: Multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) is a powerful framework for solving complex collaborative tasks, but it relies heavily on well-defined global reward functions. Designing such rewards is challenging, especially in systems with heterogeneous agents, where a single scalar objective may fail to capture diverse behaviors. In this paper, we introduce Multi-AGent Preference-Integrated lEarning (MAGPIE), which addresses these challenges through agent-specific preference modeling. Each agent is evaluated by a dedicated expert through preference signals, eliminating the need for global evaluation. We theoretically prove that optimizing these decentralized preferences converges to a Nash equilibrium policy. To integrate local preferences into a coherent global objective, we construct agent-specific reward models from preference data and combine them via a monotonic aggregation mechanism. We further prove that optimizing this aggregate reward model is equivalent to training the Nash equilibrium policy. Extensive experiments on benchmark multi-agent tasks and a sequential production line task show that MAGPIE achieves performance comparable to reward-engineered baselines, demonstrating its potential to facilitate policy learning in scenarios where precise reward engineering is impractical.

</details>


### [103] [Trajectory Design and Budgeted Querying for Digital Twin Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08631)
*Vladyslava Spitkovska,Dmytro Kuzmenko*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 数字孪生校准中，轨迹生成与有限预算下的参数查询分配可联合优化；面向激励的轨迹能显著提升参数估计精度，而查询策略在预算受限时有效维持校准性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字孪生校准依赖昂贵交互数据，现有方法未系统考虑轨迹设计（激励方向）与查询时机/预算分配之间的耦合关系，导致数据利用效率低。

Method: 融合激励导向的强化学习控制器（生成轨迹）、带预测不确定性的循环参数估计器（GRU），以及预算化查询策略；并在部分可观测环境中使用多控制器混合训练估计器。

Result: 在Pendulum中，激励轨迹下GRU估计器无查询时MAE为0.0066；三查询预算下终端误差0.0092，显著优于未校准的0.2031。在Waterworld中，混合控制器训练使在线归一化误差约4-5%。

Conclusion: 轨迹设计与查询分配应作为数据稀缺校准中的显式设计变量，尽管结果仅为探索性案例，但为后续受控消融研究提供了方向。

Abstract: Digital-twin calibration requires interaction data that is expensive to collect. We study two acquisition decisions: which trajectories to generate, and when to spend a limited budget on privileged parameter measurements. Our framework couples an excitation-oriented reinforcement learning controller, a recurrent parameter estimator with predictive uncertainty, and a budgeted query policy. In Pendulum, a Random Forest diagnostic recovers gravity only weakly from task-oriented trajectories and does not recover mass or length, while a GRU trained on excitation-oriented trajectories reaches a mean absolute error of 0.0066 with no queries. We then withdraw continuous oracle access partway through an episode, so that the twin must run on the estimator's output for the remainder. The estimator-plus-policy pipeline achieves a terminal error of 0.0092 under a three-query budget, against 0.2031 for an uncalibrated twin. In partially observable Waterworld, five controllers produce different observed error profiles across three hidden parameters, and an estimator trained on a five-controller mixture reaches online normalized errors of roughly 4-5%. These exploratory case studies are not controlled ablations, but they motivate treating trajectory design and query allocation as explicit design variables in data-scarce calibration.

</details>


### [104] [Catastrophic Forgetting in Continual Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08673)
*Emma Graham*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 本研究探讨强化学习中任务相似性与灾难性遗忘的关系，采用Q-learning在基于图的任务上实验，发现任务相似性独立影响遗忘无统计显著性，但存在复杂动态和高度变异性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灾难性遗忘是持续学习的主要障碍，理解新任务相似性如何影响旧任务性能，对设计稳健的持续学习算法至关重要，目前该关系尚不明确。

Method: 使用可解释的Q-learning在多种基于图的任务上训练，通过改变新任务的相对复杂度来量化遗忘程度，并分析任务相似性与遗忘之间的统计关系。

Result: 任务相似性与遗忘之间呈现复杂动态，遗忘严重度随相似性和复杂度波动，存在高变异性和不均匀的相似性分布，未发现任务相似性对遗忘有独立显著影响。

Conclusion: 任务相似性单独不是遗忘的显著预测因子，其作用可能与任务复杂度交织，需进一步研究以揭示潜在交互机制。

Abstract: This work explores the relationship between task similarity and catastrophic forgetting in reinforcement learning. Catastrophic forgetting, the phenomenon in machine learning of losing the ability to effectively perform on previous tasks, is a significant impediment to continual learning. This study aims to understand the extent to which the similarity of a new task influences the performance on the previous task. Interpretable reinforcement learning, specifically Q-learning, is employed on graph-based tasks with the objective of minimising the number of steps to reach a goal. The study investigates the performance on a previously learned task after training on a new task, for tasks of varying relative levels of complexity. The experimental results reveal a complex dynamic between task similarity and forgetting, with significant fluctuations in forgetting severity observed across degrees of task similarities and task complexities, and are suggestive of an interdependence of forgetting on the similarity and complexity of tasks. The observations were accompanied by observations of high degrees of variability in forgetting and an uneven distribution of task similarity measures. The relationship between these variables remains unclear and no evidence of statistical significance that task similarity has an effect, independently, on forgetting is found in continual reinforcement learning. Further research is warranted to gain a comprehensive understanding of the potential interplay between task similarity and catastrophic forgetting.

</details>


### [105] [Metanormative Theory for RL-Based Moral Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08220)
*Aleks Knoks,Marija Slavkovik*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 本文从元规范性理论视角审视强化学习（RL）在机器伦理与价值对齐中的应用，旨在为判断RL智能体行为是否具有道德性提供更清晰的标准，并据此评估比较不同RL方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器伦理与价值对齐领域日益依赖强化学习，但忽视了哲学文献的指导作用，导致缺乏对RL智能体道德属性的明确判据。

Method: 结合近期元规范性理论中的关键思想，对RL架构进行概念性分析，以此建立道德行为分类标准并构建评估框架。

Result: 获得了判断RL智能体道德地位的具体标准，以及用于比较多种RL伦理方法的理论基础。

Conclusion: 元规范性理论能为RL-based机器伦理提供重要理论支撑，有助于更严谨地设计与评估价值对齐智能体。

Abstract: The overlapping disciplines of machine ethics and value alignment are concerned with designing artificial agents that are aligned with human values and that act in ethically acceptable ways. A recent trend in these disciplines is the use of reinforcement learning (RL) to design such agents, sidelining the philosophical literature that used to play a more central role. Against this backdrop, this paper pursues two goals. The first is to draw out ideas from recent work in metanormative theory that can be useful for designing artificial moral and value-aligned agents. The second is to examine the RL architecture through the lens of these ideas. This will give us clearer criteria for when an RL agent's behavior can be classified as moral, as well as a basis for evaluating and comparing different RL-based approaches to machine ethics and value alignment.

</details>


### [106] [FemWear: A Specialized Wearable Foundation Model for Women's Health](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08244)
*Yifan Wang,Chenzhong Li*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: FemWear是一个面向女性健康的专用可穿戴基础模型，通过低秩残差适配器和因果任务头微调预训练骨干，仅训练1.11%参数，在多个女性健康任务上提升性能，但未实现通用优势或临床有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可穿戴基础模型虽跨传感器流和人群预训练，但未针对女性健康任务设计，亟需专用模型以支持月经、情绪、睡眠等预测。

Method: 保留预训练模型的patch投影和Transformer编码器，仅训练23.9万参数（占1.11%），通过低秩残差适配器和因果任务族头，学习共享纵向表示以覆盖七类女性健康结局。

Result: 在固定划分下，周期相位macro-F1提升8.15%，痉挛、情绪和睡眠问题的MAE分别降低9.32%、5.80%和9.43%；但严格留一审计中仅部分指标正向，且置信区间不严格为正，容量匹配实验中未超越GRU或MMoE基线。

Conclusion: FemWear支持针对女性健康研究的定向迁移和连贯概率输出，但未证明普遍性能优势或临床有效性，需进一步验证。

Abstract: General wearable foundation models are pretrained across broad sensor streams and populations, but are not designed around women's-health tasks. We introduce FemWear, a specialized wearable foundation model that parameter-efficiently repurposes a pretrained multimodal wearable backbone. FemWear retains the patch projection and Transformer encoder, training 239,236 parameters (1.11% of a 21.54M-parameter encoder) through low-rank residual adapters and causal task-family heads. It learns one shared longitudinal representation for menstrual, symptom, affective, sleep/recovery, autonomic, activity, and pregnancy-related outcomes. We evaluate six cohorts with 63 comparable primary metrics, including 33 from women's-health cohorts, while retaining the 32-task OpenMHC ability-retention benchmark. On a fixed participant split over three seeds, FemWear improved cycle-phase macro-F1 by 8.15% and reduced mean absolute error for cramps, mood symptoms, and sleep problems by 9.32%, 5.80%, and 9.43%, respectively. In a stricter 42-participant nested leave-one-participant-out audit, 24-hour onset, 72-hour onset, and cramps retained positive changes of 2.87%, 6.35%, and 2.19%; phase, mood, and sleep were neutral or negative, and no endpoint had a strictly positive corrected confidence interval. Capacity-matched experiments outperformed a latest-day multilayer perceptron but not shared-GRU or multi-gate mixture-of-experts baselines. Train-only calibration reduced onset expected calibration error by 84.2--88.2% with zero temporal-nesting violations. FemWear enables targeted transfer and coherent probability outputs for women's-health research, but does not establish universal performance dominance or clinical validity.

</details>


### [107] [Quantum-Classical Physics-Informed Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks for Solving Fuzzy Differential Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08782)
*Xiang Rao,Yuxuan Shen*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 提出量子-经典物理信息Kolmogorov-Arnold网络（QCPIKAN）求解模糊微分方程，融合ChebyKAN与参数化量子电路，在理想量子模拟下比PIKAN误差更低，但边界附近仍有模糊结构违例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模糊微分方程受不确定性影响，传统数值方法难以高效求解，而现有物理信息神经网络在模糊框架下精度有限；量子计算的特征表达能力有望提升求解精度。

Method: 以时空坐标和隶属度水平为联合输入，用ChebyKAN模块和参数化量子电路构建混合逼近器，同时逼近α-cut的上下端点函数，并将控制方程、初边值条件及模糊结构约束纳入损失函数；理论上建立统一误差分解框架。

Result: 在椭圆、抛物、双曲模糊方程的理想量子模拟中，QCPIKAN在多数隶属度水平下平均相对L2误差仅为PIKAN的1/2.7至1/1.1，模糊对流波前位置误差约为PIKAN的1/1.77；但两者在边界、高梯度区和波前附近仍有局部模糊结构违例。

Conclusion: QCPIKAN提供了一种高精度的量子-经典混合物理信息计算框架，可用于α-cut表示的模糊偏微分方程，但需进一步改善局部模糊结构约束的满足性。

Abstract: In this study, we propose a quantum-classical physics-informed Kolmogorov-Arnold network (QCPIKAN) dedicated to the solution of fuzzy differential equations. The network takes the spatiotemporal coordinates and membership level as joint inputs and employs ChebyKAN modules and a parameterized quantum circuit to construct a hybrid function approximator. It simultaneously approximates the lower and upper endpoint functions associated with the α-cuts and incorporates the governing equations, initial-boundary conditions, and fuzzy-structural constraints into the training objective. Theoretically, a unified error-analysis framework is established for QCPIKAN and PIKAN, in which the endpoint-solution error is decomposed into approximation, sampling, optimization, and fuzzy-structure constraint errors. Under the assumptions of well-posedness and residual stability, it is proved that QCPIKAN has a smaller a priori error bound when the representational gain introduced by quantum entanglement features exceeds the additional computational error. Numerical experiments are conducted for elliptic, parabolic, and hyperbolic equations in an ideal quantum-simulation environment. The results show that QCPIKAN captures the overall contraction of the solution interval as increases. At most tested membership levels, the mean relative L2 error of PIKAN is approximately 1.1-2.7 times that of QCPIKAN. In the fuzzy convection example, the mean wavefront-position error of PIKAN is approximately 1.77 times that of QCPIKAN. Nevertheless, both models still exhibit local fuzzy-structure violations near boundaries, in high-gradient regions, and around the wavefront. These results indicate that QCPIKAN provides a quantum-classical hybrid physics-informed computational framework with comparatively high predictive accuracy for solving fuzzy partial differential equations represented by α-cuts.

</details>


### [108] [Hybrid Neural-Classical Correction for Frozen Time Series Foundation Models: A Comprehensive Ablation Study on High-Frequency Stock Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08825)
*Kasun Dewage,Suranadi De Silva,Shankhadeep Mondal*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 通过混合神经-经典残差学习，仅用49K参数的门控线性模型即可将冻结的TimesFM在股票日内预测上的相关性提升6.4倍，经典随机森林残差学习贡献最大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础时间序列模型在金融高频数据上表现不佳，需要有效的领域适配方法，但现有研究缺乏对神经与经典组件协同作用的系统分析。

Method: 在冻结的TimesFM（200M）之上，对比AttnCorrect（471K）和GatedLinear（49K）两种神经校正头，并分别叠加随机森林残差学习；在10只科技股、200万数据点上进行消融实验，采用三种互补相关性指标评估。

Result: 混合方法达到0.597合并相关性，日均相关性提升6.4倍；随机森林单独贡献最大且可匹敌或超越神经组件；移除经典残差后，简单架构（GatedLinear）反超复杂架构（AttnCorrect）；自注意力是神经组件中贡献最大的。GatedLinear+RF以9倍更少参数取得最佳性能。

Conclusion: 有效的基础模型适配需要精心整合神经与经典方法，经典残差学习是不可或缺的补充；在资源受限场景下，轻量门控线性校正加随机森林是最优选择。

Abstract: Foundation models for time series forecasting demonstrate impressive zero-shot generalization but often underperform on specialized domains such as high-frequency finance. We present a comprehensive study of hybrid neural-classical correction for adapting frozen TimesFM (200M parameters) to stock return prediction during the volatile opening trading hour. We compare two neural correction architectures - AttnCorrect (multi-head self-attention, approximately 471K parameters) and GatedLinear (low-rank bilinear projection with gating, approximately 49K parameters) - each augmented with Random Forest residual learning. Through systematic ablation across 10 major technology stocks (NVDA, MSFT, AAPL, GOOG, GOOGL, AMZN, META, AVGO, TSLA, NFLX) spanning 2 million data points, we reveal critical insights: (1) The hybrid neural-classical approach achieves 0.597 pooled correlation and 6.4x mean per-day correlation improvement over frozen TimesFM; (2) Classical residual learning (Random Forest) provides the largest single-component contribution, matching or exceeding the neural correction component; (3) Simpler neural architectures surprisingly outperform complex ones when classical residual learning is removed; (4) Self-attention provides the largest neural-only contribution. GatedLinear+RF achieves best overall performance with 9x fewer neural parameters than AttnCorrect+RF. We report three complementary correlation metrics - mean per-day, cross-day cumulative, and pooled - to provide a complete picture of predictive quality. Our results provide practical guidance: effective foundation model adaptation requires careful integration of neural and classical components, with classical methods playing a crucial complementary role.

</details>


### [109] [Beyond Routing: Decoupling Expert Dispatch and Aggregation in Sparse Mixture-of-Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08853)
*Zongfei Li*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 本文质疑稀疏MoE中路由器的双重角色——选择专家与聚合专家输出——是否应当解耦。通过冻结预训练模型和路由，仅训练轻量级后计算头（FDAA），在OLMoE和DeepSeek-V2-Lite上显著提升语言建模性能，表明专家选择与输出聚合可以分离并独立优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MoE路由器通常用同一分数既选择专家又加权其输出，但缺乏证据表明该耦合是最优的。作者希望验证：在固定专家选择与计算的前提下，仅改变聚合方式是否能带来收益，从而揭示选择与聚合两个功能间的潜在冲突。

Method: 首先在预训练OLMoE-1B-7B上进行结构化oracle审计：固定Top-8专家ID、计算量和总路由质量，仅调整集合内聚合权重，测量交叉熵变化及路由器分数与最优权重间的相关性。随后提出FDAA：冻结主干、路由器和专家，只训练一个301K参数的后计算头，直接用语言建模目标优化。在DeepSeek-V2-Lite（Top-6+共享专家）上复制审计并训练FDAA，进行混合领域训练和冻结评估。

Result: OLMoE上oracle聚合改善full-horizon CE 0.0160±0.0039，路由器Top1专家仅有17.2%概率为反事实最优顶点，Spearman相关性仅0.030。FDAA在WikiText-103上Delta CE=-0.1523±0.0031，混合领域训练在WikiText、C4和PTB上均获益。DeepSeek-V2-Lite审计显示最佳顶点头寸仍显著，Top1识别最优专家比例仅12.5%/16.7%；FDAA复制实验中WikiText和PTB提升，C4统计中性。

Conclusion: 实验证实MoE中专家选择与专家承诺（聚合权重）应被视为独立功能。跨架构（OLMoE和DeepSeek-V2-Lite）一致的证据表明：轻量级后计算头能有效优化聚合，且与路由器的选择分数解耦，为改进MoE推理提供低成本新途径。

Abstract: Sparse Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) routers commonly use the same scores both to select experts and to weight their already-computed outputs. We study whether these two roles, dispatch and aggregation, should be coupled. On pretrained OLMoE-1B-7B, we keep selected Top-8 expert IDs, expert computation, and total selected router mass fixed and change only within-set aggregation. A structured oracle improves full-horizon cross-entropy by 0.0160 +/- 0.0039 across three seeds; the router's top-scored expert is the counterfactual-best vertex only 17.2% of the time, with router-utility Spearman 0.030. We therefore train Fixed-Dispatch Adaptive Aggregation (FDAA), a 301K-parameter post-compute head optimized directly with the language-modeling objective while freezing the backbone, router, and experts. On OLMoE, FDAA improves fresh WikiText-103 test by Delta CE = -0.1523 +/- 0.0031 across three seeds, and mixed-domain training gives robust gains on WikiText-103, C4, and held-out Penn Treebank under frozen confirmatory evaluation. We also replicate the fixed-dispatch audit on DeepSeek-V2-Lite, which uses Top-6 routed experts plus shared experts. Best-vertex headroom remains significant on WikiText and C4, while router Top1 identifies the best selected expert in only 12.5% and 16.7% of audited examples. In a one-seed mixed-domain replication, FDAA improves locked WikiText and PTB, while C4 is statistically neutral. These results support a cross-architecture distinction between expert selection and expert commitment.

</details>


### [110] [A Domain-Structured Ensemble Framework for Perioperative Outcome Prediction Using Electronic Health Record Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08920)
*Shikhar Shukla,Cristina Barboi*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 提出一种基于领域结构化集成框架的围手术期结局预测模型，利用常规EHR数据，通过域特定梯度提升模型与逻辑回归元学习器整合，显著提升术后谵妄预测性能与校准度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有围手术期风险预测模型受限于狭窄手术人群、不完整的术中数据、校准不佳及可解释性差，亟需一种通用且可扩展的预测框架。

Method: 构建患者相关、手术相关和麻醉相关三个领域的预测因子，分别训练梯度提升模型生成独立风险估计，再通过逻辑回归元学习器进行集成，并在包含5386例手术事件（病例对照设计）的州级健康信息交换数据上验证，排除术前痴呆患者，以ICD编码和CAM阳性定义POD。

Result: 堆叠元学习器AUROC达0.899，PR-AUC 0.881，Brier分数0.126，优于最佳单阶段模型（AUROC 0.849）；域消融实验显示优于仅手术模型（AUROC 0.879，Brier 0.140）；时间验证AUROC为0.915；校准截距-0.006，斜率1.035；决策曲线分析在临床合理阈值内净获益为正。

Conclusion: 该模块化框架支持替代结局、扩展预测域和动态风险更新，为可解释且校准良好的围手术期临床决策支持提供了可扩展基础。

Abstract: Perioperative risk prediction models are often limited by narrow surgical populations, incomplete intraoperative data, poor calibration, and limited interpretability. We present a domain-structured ensemble framework for perioperative outcome prediction using routinely collected electronic health record (EHR) data. Predictors are organized into patient-related, surgery-related, and anesthetics-related domains. Domain-specific gradient boosting models generate independent risk estimates that are integrated through a logistic regression meta-learner. We demonstrate the framework using postoperative delirium (POD) in a case-control sample of 5,386 surgical encounters (2,693 cases, 2,693 controls) from a statewide health information exchange. POD required both delirium-related ICD codes and a positive Confusion Assessment Method screening within seven postoperative days; patients with preexisting dementia were excluded. The stacked meta-learner achieved AUROC 0.899 (95% CI: 0.891-0.906), precision-recall AUC 0.881, and Brier score 0.126, compared with AUROC 0.849 for the best single-stage model. Domain ablation showed improved discrimination and calibration over a surgery-only model (AUROC 0.879, Brier 0.140). Temporal validation on held-out post-2017 data yielded AUROC 0.915. Calibration was excellent, with intercept -0.006 (95% CI: -0.083 to 0.070) and slope 1.035 (95% CI: 0.982 to 1.088). Decision curve analysis, corrected for case-control sampling, showed positive net benefit across clinically plausible thresholds. The modular framework supports alternative outcomes, extension of predictor domains, and dynamic risk updating, providing a scalable foundation for interpretable, calibration-aware perioperative clinical decision support.

</details>


### [111] [Label-Free Parkinson's Disease Screening from Face and Voice through Mechanistic Interpretability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08976)
*Jiaheng Su,Yu Sun*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 提出一种无需PD标签的帕金森病筛查方法，仅用冻结的预训练编码器（表情ViT和HuBERT）处理面部与语音，通过合成退化对比激活添加和异常检测实现无监督筛查，融合后AUROC为0.802。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器学习筛查依赖PD标签，但数据受隐私和专家标注限制，因此希望开发完全无标签的筛查方法，利用预训练模型和健康对照数据即可工作。

Method: 语音侧用时间拉伸和气息退化生成合成构音障碍，构建CAA方向；面部侧用k近邻异常分数偏离对照嵌入簇；最后等权晚期融合，并用对齐原则（合成与真实疾病方向余弦相似度）验证CAA适用性。

Result: 语音CAA余弦+0.37（AUROC 0.765），面部CAA余弦-0.48但异常检测有效（AUROC 0.751），融合AUROC 0.802且NPV 0.95；语音侧无过拟合，面部及融合结果可能偏乐观。

Conclusion: 无标签PD筛查可行，语音CAA方法有效，面部异常检测补充，融合支持阴性排除分诊；但面部结果需外部验证，对齐原则为解释合成退化方法提供依据。

Abstract: Parkinson's disease (PD) is the second most common neurodegenerative disorder. Typical machine learning screening methods require PD labels, but the available data is limited by privacy concerns and the need for expert annotation. We propose a label-free face-plus-voice PD screen built entirely on frozen pretrained encoders--a face-expression Vision Transformer and HuBERT--in which no PD label touches any fit; the reference is training controls only. The voice modality uses a synthetic-dysarthria contrastive activation addition (CAA) direction built from time-stretch and breathy degradation of healthy speech; the face modality uses a k-nearest-neighbor anomaly score to the control embedding cluster. We introduce the alignment principle, a post-hoc analysis showing that a synthetic-degradation CAA detector works when the cosine similarity between the synthetic and real disease directions exceeds zero. Measured on the YouTubePD benchmark, this cosine is +0.37 for voice (CAA works, AUROC 0.765) and -0.48 for face (CAA fails; anomaly succeeds, AUROC 0.751). Equal-weight late fusion reaches AUROC 0.802 (95% CI [0.70,0.89]) with NPV 0.95, supporting a rule-out triage interpretation. An overfitting audit shows the voice detector transfers cleanly, while the face-side--and thus fused--AUROC is potentially optimistic pending external validation.

</details>


### [112] [Twin Rollouts: Noise-Coupled Counterfactual Branching in Interactive Video World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08982)
*Yu Ma,Hongli Shi,Xinran Xu*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 本文提出在交互式视频世界模型的生成轨迹中进行反事实生成，通过噪声耦合孪生轨迹实现精确反事实推理，并定义时空局部性度量作为可验证属性，用于后续训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频世界模型仅在前向事实预测上训练和评估，忽略了生成轨迹内部的反事实推理能力，而反事实能力对规划和决策至关重要。

Method: 形式化噪声耦合孪生轨迹：事实分支与反事实分支共享生成前缀和未来外生噪声序列，仅在干预点后的动作流不同；利用自生成轨迹使珍珠反事实程序中的溯因步骤精确，并以模拟器地面真值计算时空局部性度量作为奖励进行后训练。

Result: 实验尚未进行，本文目前仅建立形式化框架、度量定义和定位。

Conclusion: 该工作为视频世界模型的反事实生成提供了理论基础和可验证评估方法，实验验证留待后续工作。

Abstract: Interactive video world models generate rollouts autoregressively under an action stream, yet they are trained and evaluated almost exclusively on factual prediction. We study counterfactual generation inside the rollout: given a trajectory the model has itself generated, what would have happened had the actions differed from step t* onward? We formalize noise-coupled twin rollouts --- a factual and a counterfactual branch sharing the generated prefix and the future exogenous noise sequence, diverging only in the action stream at an intervention point. Because the factual branch is self-generated, its exogenous noise is known exactly: the abduction step of Pearl's counterfactual procedure is exact by construction, sidestepping the approximate-inversion problem faced by editing-based pipelines. Noise coupling further turns the minimal-change principle into a per-sample verifiable property: we define a spatiotemporal locality metric that penalizes divergence outside the causal descendants of the intervention, computable against simulator ground truth without a learned judge. Forking the simulator state at t* yields ground-truth counterfactual re-renders, which we use as verifiable rewards for post-training. This note establishes the formal framework, metric definitions, and positioning; experiments are forthcoming.

</details>


### [113] [SoftMCC: An MCC-Brier Calibration Bridge for Threshold-Free Model Selection under Class Imbalance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08984)
*Özkan Canay*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: SoftMCC 是一种基于概率的 MCC 验证框架，通过协方差归一化的概率-标签关联评分，在排名稳定性上优于 AUPRC 和 MCC@0.5，但对校准敏感且不提升选定模型的效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统 MCC 因阈值化导致验证排名依赖阈值，降低模型选择的可重复性；需要一种摆脱固定阈值、保留 MCC 特性的验证指标。

Method: 利用概率值混淆计数，结合 MCC 特定的校准恒等式和 tie-aware 共享池选择协议，核心评分为协方差归一化的概率-标签关联，并在完美校准下等价于 Brier skill score。

Result: 在 18 个设置中，SoftMCC 获得最佳稳定性平均排名 (2.31) 和最高 Kendall W (0.659)，Friedman 检验显著 (p=0.007)；但效用分析无优势，且对标签置换和温度缩放敏感。

Conclusion: SoftMCC 是一个校准敏感的 MCC 族模型选择器，稳定性有界，但效用证据不足，不适合作为通用替代指标。

Abstract: Model selection for imbalanced binary classification often uses the Matthews correlation coefficient (MCC), but thresholding makes validation rankings threshold-dependent. SoftMCC is a post-training MCC validation framework on established probability-valued confusion counts, coupling an MCC-specific calibrated identity with a tie-aware, shared-pool selection protocol. Its core score is a covariance-normalized probability-label association, reduces exactly to MCC for hard predictions, and is Pearson-bounded. Under perfect population calibration it equals the Brier skill score with identical candidate ordering; outside that regime the gap does not identify calibration error. Across 18 settings with 12 duplicate-safe grouped repeats, SoftMCC attains the best stability mean rank (2.31) and highest mean tie-corrected Kendall's W (0.659), with a significant Friedman test (p=0.007); Nemenyi analysis separates it from AUPRC and MCC@0.5, while 14-source-family sensitivity retains only the latter. Selected-model utility shows no advantage. Three of six prespecified comparisons have negative mean test-MCC differences, only F1@best survives Holm correction (p=0.014), and the dataset-level test is not significant (p=0.117). Label permutation lowers mean W to 0.092; temperature scaling shifts SoftMCC rankings (mean Spearman 0.851) whereas rank-based and threshold-optimized metrics remain invariant. SoftMCC is a calibration-sensitive MCC-family selector with bounded stability and utility evidence.

</details>


### [114] [F2STNet: Fair and Federated Spectral-Temporal Modeling for Graph Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09082)
*Jiayi Zhang,Jinfeng Xu,Hewei Wang,Siyuan Cen,Haidong Huang,Yiyao Zhan,Zheyu Chen,Jinjiang You,Ai Jian,Edith C. H. Ngai*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 提出联邦时空预测框架F²STNet，结合截断图傅里叶特征、轻量状态空间时序编码器与公平感知联邦聚合，在保持线性复杂度的同时提升预测精度与客户端公平性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图结构时空预测面临数据分散、异构性挑战，传统集中式方法难以兼顾序列建模与协同训练，且联邦聚合易受客户端数据分布差异影响导致公平性下降。

Method: 设计频谱分支提取图频率结构，采用对角状态空间层以线性复杂度建模长时依赖，结合图卷积融合时空信息；提出公平感知联邦聚合FFA，基于客户端验证损失和递增公平调度调整FedAvg聚合权重。

Result: 在PeMS04、HZMetro、KnowAir数据集上预测精度优于对比基线；PeMS04联邦实验进一步改善最差客户端性能并降低客户端间指标分散度。

Conclusion: 验证了频谱特征+状态空间编码+公平聚合在联邦时空预测中的有效性，为异构分布式图数据场景提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Spatiotemporal prediction on graph-structured data is central to traffic forecasting and environmental monitoring, yet decentralized and heterogeneous data complicate both sequence modeling and collaborative training. We propose F$^2$STNet, a federated forecasting framework that combines truncated graph-Fourier features, a lightweight diagonal state-space temporal encoder, graph convolution, and Fairness-aware Federated Aggregation (FFA). The spectral branch exposes graph-frequency structure, while the state-space layer models long temporal dependencies with linear complexity in the sequence length. FFA adjusts the FedAvg prior using client validation losses and an increasing fairness schedule. Experiments on PeMS04, HZMetro, and KnowAir show favorable forecasting accuracy relative to the evaluated baselines; federated experiments on PeMS04 additionally improve worst-client and client-dispersion metrics.

</details>


### [115] [Real Data Closes Synthetic-to-Real Gap in Optical Chemical Structure Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09100)
*Yani Guan,Dengpan Dong,Zi Wei,Shuang Luo,Dan Hannah,Yumin Zhang,Kang Xu*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 在真实化学结构识别中，使用真实标注数据进行微调是提升效果的关键，且基础模型与真实数据混合比例需联合选择；视觉塔LoRA的效果因基础模型而异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OCSR在合成图像上表现良好但在真实文档上性能骤降（如Qwen2.5-VL-7B从91%降至16%），需系统识别改进来源，以缩小合成到真实的差距。

Method: 对21个识别器进行微调，系统改变VLM基础模型、真实训练数据比例和视觉塔适应策略，并跨化学（ACS、CLEF-IP、USPTO等）及非化学任务进行受控对比。

Result: 真实数据比例显著提升准确率（Qwen从0.15到0.46）；视觉塔LoRA对Qwen无效（+0.00），对InternVL3显著（+22.8至+34.6），对GLM中等；最佳配置在清洁渲染上0.96，在四个真实基准上为0.49、0.65、0.84、0.76；基础模型间差距随真实数据增加而缩小并改变排序。

Conclusion: 视觉结构识别的模型与适应策略选择必须基于目标任务数据评估，不能仅依赖合成基准；基础模型与真实数据混合需协同优化。

Abstract: Millions of chemical structures appear in patents and papers only as drawings, and using that information at scale requires reading the drawings. OCSR appears nearly solved on synthetic images yet remains difficult on real documents: the starting recognizer, Qwen2.5-VL-7B, exceeds 91% accuracy on synthetic renders but falls below 16% on three real-world benchmarks (ACS, CLEF-IP, USPTO). To identify the main source of improvement, 21 recognizers were fine-tuned on mixtures of synthetically rendered structures and labeled real depictions from patents, journal figures, and hand-drawn collections, varying the vision language model (VLM) base, the fraction of real training data, and the vision-tower adaptation strategy. Labeled real training images make the largest difference. For Qwen2.5-VL, ACS exact match rises from 0.15 with no real data to 0.37 at 9.5% and 0.46 at 50.2%; a controlled experiment across three base models reproduces the trend. A vision-tower LoRA, in contrast, does nothing for Qwen (+0.00, paired p=1.00), substantially helps InternVL3-8B (+22.8 to +34.6 pt), and modestly helps GLM-4.1V-9B (+1.0 to +9.6 pt), so its value depends on the base model. The best configuration reaches 0.96 exact match on clean renders and 0.49, 0.65, 0.84, and 0.76 on ACS, CLEF-IP, UOB, and USPTO, respectively. Gaps between base models are largest without real data (0.21), shrink to 0.06 at 70% real data, and reorder the ranking; base model and real-data mixture must therefore be selected together. Small-scale experiments on handwritten image-to-LaTeX recognition and chart-to-table conversion show that base-model rankings also vary beyond chemistry. More generally, model and adaptation choices for visual structure recognition should be evaluated on the target task.

</details>


### [116] [SDDBMs: Soft Denoising Diffusion Bridge Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08594)
*Shiyi Qi,Kun He,Mingmou Liu*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 提出软去噪扩散桥模型（SDDBMs），通过将终端硬约束替换为非退化高斯终端边际，消除终端边界奇异性，提升图像恢复的数值稳定性与生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散桥模型依赖硬端点约束，导致终端分布退化为Dirac测度，漂移系数在端点附近病态，引发数值不稳定等问题。

Method: 在路径测度变换中预设非退化高斯终端边际（灵活中心与方差），推导含高斯终端重加权和软h函数的闭合形式软桥，获得无x0依赖的高斯前向边际与动力学，统一覆盖DDBMs、GOUB、UniDB等特例。

Result: 在图像恢复任务中，SDDBMs相比现有桥方法获得更优数值稳定性和更高生成质量。

Conclusion: 软终端约束是缓解桥模型边界奇异性的有效泛化框架，兼具理论统一性与实践优势。

Abstract: Diffusion bridge models leverage Doob's \(h\)-transform to construct stochastic transports between arbitrary endpoint distributions, and have shown strong potential in image-to-image translation and restoration. However, most existing bridge models rely on hard endpoint conditioning, which forces the terminal state to match a prescribed target exactly. This hard constraint induces terminal-boundary singularities: the terminal law collapses to a Dirac measure, and the resulting drift coefficients become ill-conditioned near the endpoint. In this paper, we propose Soft Denoising Diffusion Bridge Models (SDDBMs), a generalized framework that regularizes diffusion bridges directly at the level of their terminal constraints. Instead of imposing an exact endpoint, SDDBMs prescribe a non-degenerate Gaussian terminal marginal under the transformed path measure, with a flexible terminal center and variance. Starting from this prescribed marginal, we develop a complete closed-form construction of the soft bridge, including the Gaussian terminal reweighting and soft \(h\)-function, the induced Gaussian forward marginals and \(\mathbf{x}_0\)-free dynamics. Theoretically, SDDBMs provide a unified probabilistic perspective that encompasses existing diffusion bridge models, including DDBMs, GOUB, and UniDB, as special cases under specific parameter choices. Extensive experiments on image restoration tasks demonstrate that SDDBMs achieve improved numerical stability and superior generation quality over existing bridge-based methods.

</details>


### [117] [Walking through Discussions: A Mobile Visual Analytics System for In-Situ Group Discussion Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08617)
*Yiping Sun,Ziyao Kang,Wei Zeng,Minli Wu,Jiazhi Xia*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 提出移动端可视化分析系统MobileGroupVis，支持教师在实体课堂走动教学中实时监控多个小组讨论、快速诊断目标组问题并进行干预。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工具主要支持桌面端的事后分析，缺乏对教师现场走动教学情境的实时支持，导致教师难以及时监控多组讨论并快速定位需介入的小组。

Method: 采用轻量级流式分析流水线将小组音频转为结构化讨论数据，通过对话分析模块提取交互模式、话题进展和话题偏离；设计六个协调视图（紧凑字形总览+细节视图）适配小屏触控交互，集成多组监控、单组诊断与教学干预工作流。

Result: 通过两个案例研究和专家访谈，初步验证系统能帮助教师理解讨论过程、识别需关注小组并促进课堂实时干预。

Conclusion: MobileGroupVis为移动端课堂小组讨论的现场分析提供了可行方案，但需进一步扩大评估以验证其泛化性与长期教学效果。

Abstract: Group discussion-based teaching is widely used to foster collaborative learning, yet teachers in physical classrooms often struggle to simultaneously monitor multiple groups and quickly diagnose a target group before intervening. Existing visual analytics tools primarily support post-hoc analysis on desktop, providing limited support for in-situ walk-around teaching. To address this gap, we present MobileGroupVis, a mobile visual analytics system for in-situ analysis of classroom group discussions. MobileGroupVis integrates multi-group monitoring, single-group diagnosis, and instructional intervention into a concise analytical workflow tailored for small-screen touch interaction. The system is powered by a lightweight streaming analysis pipeline that converts group audio into structured discussion data and further extracts interaction patterns, topic progression, and topic deviation through a dialogue analysis module. To enable both glanceable overview and traceable diagnosis, we design six coordinated views, including a compact glyph that visually encodes word count, interaction intensity, and topic deviation for efficient cross-group comparison and anomaly localization, along with detailed views for opinion evolution, interaction dynamics, topic coverage, and dialogue records. We evaluate MobileGroupVis through two case studies and expert interviews. The results provide preliminary evidence that MobileGroupVis supports teachers in understanding discussion processes, identifying groups in need of attention, and facilitating in-class intervention.

</details>


### [118] [Business Arena: Benchmarking LLM Agents in a Realistic Marketplace](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08621)
*Yijun Pan,Yukun Lian,Kunyu Shi,Junbo Li,Hongwei Xue,Sicong Xie,Guannan Zhang,Xiaoying Xing*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 提出 Business Arena，一个基于真实数据的长周期跨境电商经营环境，用于评估 LLM 智能体的商业能力；15 个前沿模型表现差距达 9 倍，最佳模型仍落后于人类策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体基准很少评估商业运营能力，而经营企业需要从不完整信号中推断机会、在不确定下配置资本、适应延迟反馈并满足合规要求，这些复杂能力缺乏受控且可量化的测试环境。

Method: 构建受控跨境店铺环境，使用真实 Alibaba.com 货源数据与权威校准的市场条件；通过人类设计策略对比估算机会空间，采用技能级指标和多层级归因（利润→技能→行动）分析表现，并用机制消融排除捷径。

Result: 评估 15 个前沿模型，最终净资产均值相差 9 倍；最佳模型仍逊于人类策略；技能分析显示不同经营风格（高利润、高周转、客服型），行动归因定位到选品、定价和补救决策。

Conclusion: Business Arena 作为首个端到端商业智能体可信测试平台，表明当前 LLM 在真实商业运营中仍有显著差距，并为诊断能力短板和设计改进提供细粒度工具。

Abstract: Running a business is a challenging form of intelligent work. Operators must infer opportunities from partial signals, commit capital under uncertainty, adapt to delayed outcomes in a changing market, and satisfy regulatory obligations before trading legally. Frontier LLM agents can increasingly complete complex workflows, yet business-related capabilities are rarely evaluated in existing agent benchmarks. We introduce \textbf{Business Arena}, a controlled environment where an AI agent runs a cross-border shop, buying from suppliers and selling to buyers over a long horizon. We ground the arena in real Alibaba.com sourcing data and market conditions calibrated from authoritative sources. Delayed and coupled consequences make individual business decisions difficult to judge, but their combined outcome is measurable through profit. Because profit alone cannot explain why an agent succeeds or fails, we compare agents with human-designed strategies to estimate available opportunity, use skill-level metrics to reveal underlying strengths and weaknesses, and trace realized gains and losses to the actions that produced them. We use mechanism ablations to establish that strong results reflect genuine business intelligence rather than neglect or simulator-specific shortcuts. We evaluate 15 frontier models and find a ninefold difference in mean final net worth. Even the best model falls behind human-designed strategies, indicating that business operation remains challenging for LLM agents. Skill-level analysis reveals operating styles, from margin-focused premium sellers to high-turnover wholesalers and customer-service specialists, while action-level attribution identifies the sourcing, pricing, and recovery decisions that create or destroy value. Together, Business Arena takes a first step toward a realistic and trustworthy testbed for evaluating end-to-end business agents.

</details>


### [119] [DreOPD: Degraded-Reference Extrapolative On-Policy Distillation for Flow-matching Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09233)
*Mingfeng Lin,Chengfei Cai,Lin Xu,Yuxiang Wei,Liang Han*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 提出DreOPD方法，将隐式奖励外推转化为闭式速度回归，结合退化参考增强对比，实现流匹配模型的高效后训练，在单/多教师设置下超越OPD和RL基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流匹配模型的后训练面临目标冲突和梯度方差高的问题，现有OPD虽稳定但仅模仿教师，缺乏外推能力，需兼顾稳定性和奖励优化。

Method: 通过构建轻度退化参考，将奖励外推转化为速度回归的闭式解，利用教师与退化参考的对比方向指导学生更新，保持OPD的密集监督特性。

Result: 在单教师和多教师场景中，平均性能优于OPD和多任务RL基线，且在多数指标上超过专用教师模型。

Conclusion: DreOPD有效弥合了模仿学习和奖励优化之间的鸿沟，为流匹配模型提供稳定且可外推的后训练范式。
