Abstract: Reasoning modes of language models outperform their non-reasoning counterparts on multi-step agentic tasks, but pay a 3-6x premium in output tokens on every episode -- much of it spent re-deriving procedures that are shared across episodes of the same domain. We show this recurring cost can be amortized: a coding agent analyses a small corpus of existing trajectories from a training split and compiles a compact natural-language skill that is injected into the non-reasoning model's system prompt. Across four agentic benchmarks (ALFWorld, tau$^2$-bench telecom and retail, and SpreadsheetBench-Verified), skills recover 55%-100%+ of the reasoning gap for GPT-5.4-mini on held-out tasks -- exceeding the reasoning mode outright on two of four -- while emitting 2.7-6x fewer output tokens and zero reasoning tokens. Notably, reasoning traces are not a prerequisite: skills distilled from non-reasoning trajectories alone remain competitive with skills distilled from paired reasoning/non-reasoning corpora, with domain-dependent differences between the two sources. We interpret these results through a search lens: test-time reasoning is deep search inside a single episode, re-paid at every deployment, while corpus distillation is wide search across episodes, paid once. The two recover overlapping procedural knowledge, and width over cheap trajectories is often the better buy -- with the residual gap on some domains (telecom, SpreadsheetBench) delineating where genuinely per-instance deep search remains necessary.

</details>


### [190] [TelemetrySuffBench: Is Agent Telemetry Sufficient for Failure-Origin Diagnosis?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07899)
*Yuxuan Zhu,Peng Pu*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出TelemetrySuffBench基准，系统评估大语言模型在遥测数据上的故障检测、根源定位与安全弃权能力，发现检测与定位之间存在显著差距，且模型间差异大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Agent系统遥测虽能显示失败，但难以定位失败源头，缺乏将检测、定位与安全弃权分离的受控基准。

Method: 构建多组件规范轨迹，引入延迟绑定故障，生成粗视图、七因素遥测掩码和精确等义模糊对，统一评估五个前沿语言模型，含明确候选集、无效输出计分、子组分析和冻结盲测集。

Result: 完整遥测下起源步骤Top-1准确率从33.8%到97.2%；元数据、OTel及OpenInference视图保持99.5%-100%检测F1但定位准确率至多0.5%；移除决策内容使定位准确率归零；证据门控使三模型弃权能力提升12.5-48.6个百分点，两模型完全依赖；盲测复现核心模式。

Conclusion: 终端状态足以支持检测，但可靠因果定位必须依赖明确的决策到来源链接，且弃权保护机制需跨模型稳健；数据集和实现已公开。

Abstract: Agent systems increasingly expose execution traces, yet telemetry that reveals a failure may still be inadequate for identifying where that failure originated. We introduce TelemetrySuffBench, a controlled benchmark that separates failure detection, fault-origin localization, and safe abstention under insufficient evidence. The benchmark constructs canonical multi-component traces with delayed-binding faults and renders them as paired coarse views, seven-factor telemetry masks, and exact-equal ambiguous origin pairs. We evaluate five frontier language models using unified protocols, explicit candidate sets, invalid-output accounting, subgroup analyses, and a frozen blind holdout. With full telemetry, origin-step Top-1 accuracy ranges from 33.8% to 97.2% across models. Metadata, OpenTelemetry-compatible, and OpenInference-compatible views retain 99.5% to 100% detection F1 while limiting origin-step accuracy to at most 0.5%, exposing a robust detection-localization gap. Factor ablations further show that removing decision content reduces origin-step accuracy to zero for every model, while provenance removal also causes large model-dependent losses. On rich ambiguous inputs that require abstention, evidence gating reduces unsupported unique-origin answers by 12.5 to 48.6 percentage points for three models, whereas two models still answer every case, revealing strong model dependence in safe abstention. Results on the frozen holdout reproduce the central pattern within the same generator family. These findings show that terminal status can support detection, whereas reliable causal attribution requires explicit decision-to-provenance links and abstention safeguards that remain effective across models. The dataset and benchmark implementation are available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/TelemetrySuffBench-E635/README.md.

</details>


### [191] [GraphThink: Graph-Enhanced LLM Thinking for Long-Horizon Embodied Task Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07905)
*Chen Li,Sijie Cheng,Yuelin Zhang,Junxi Li,Maozhi Huang,Yang Liu,Wenbing Huang*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: GraphThink 通过任务图与场景图双图机制，显著提升具身智能体的规划鲁棒性、长程任务泛化与环境感知能力，在 ALFRED 基准上达到最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于 LLM 的具身规划器存在物理幻觉、长程任务泛化差及环境感知不足三大缺陷，亟需结构化知识注入与闭环纠错机制。

Method: 构建任务图提供结构化规划知识，以上下文提示与迭代精炼引导 LLM 推理；结合 GRPO 框架设计细粒度奖励以训练长程规划能力；引入基于场景图的事件驱动重规划模块，实现闭环环境感知与错误修正。

Result: 在 ALFRED 基准上取得最优结果，高层规划器在验证集与长程任务上超越主流 API 级 LLM，并展现出强大的零样本、少样本及分布外泛化能力。

Conclusion: 双图协同框架有效解决了 LLM 具身规划的固有短板，验证了结构化知识增强与事件驱动重规划的可行性，为鲁棒具身决策提供了新范式。

Abstract: Embodied agents using LLM-based planners often struggle with physical hallucinations, poor generalization to long-horizon tasks, and lack of environmental awareness. We propose GraphThink, a novel framework that integrates a task graph to provide structured knowledge for robust planning and a scene graph to maintain environmental memory for event-driven replanning. Specifically, the task graph guides LLM thinking through contextual prompting and iterative refinement, effectively mitigating planning hallucinations. Furthermore, within the GRPO framework, the task graph offers delicate reward design to train the LLM planner, enhancing long-horizon planning capabilities and improving generalization. Finally, an event-driven replanning module, powered by the scene graph, enables closed-loop environment awareness and error correction. GraphThink achieves state-of-the-art performance on the ALFRED benchmark. In particular, our high-level planner surpasses leading API-based LLMs on both the validation set and held-out long-horizon tasks, underscoring its robust zero-shot and few-shot capabilities. Additional evaluations further demonstrate strong out-of-distribution generalization to novel tasks and environments.

</details>


### [192] [TongGuOCR: A Layout-Aware and Token-Augmented OCR Framework for Chinese Historical Documents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07917)
*Zhongheng Zhou,Yi Sun,Huiguo He,Yuyi Zhang,Peirong Zhang,Yulin Fang,Dezhi Peng,Minghui Liao,Lianwen Jin*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出TongGuOCR，一种针对中文历史文档的布局感知与标记增强OCR框架，在基准测试中显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中文历史文档的扫描图像难以进行全文检索和计算分析，而现有OCR因复杂布局、生僻字和复杂阅读顺序而准确率不足。

Method: 包含布局感知预处理模块（构建局部一致识别块）和标记增强识别模块（字符级词汇扩展+行间转移建模注入空间位移标记）。

Result: 在M5HisDoc基准上AR达93.76，NED从10.43降至6.15，RO-ED从7.53降至3.49，优于传统OCR和MLLM。

Conclusion: TongGuOCR有效解决了中文历史文档OCR的布局和生僻字难题，并为后续数字人文研究提供了更精准的全文转录工具。

Abstract: Chinese historical documents preserve valuable cultural heritage, but many collections remain accessible only as scanned page images, preventing full-text retrieval, collation, and computational analysis. Optical character recognition (OCR) can bridge this gap, but accurate transcription remains challenging because historical documents often contain complex layouts, rare characters, and nontrivial reading orders. We propose TongGuOCR, a layout-aware and token-augmented OCR framework for Chinese historical documents. First, a Layout-Aware Preprocessing module constructs and refines locally coherent recognition blocks to preserve local context while reducing interference across regions. Second, a Token-Augmented Recognition module augments the transcription target at two complementary levels: character-level vocabulary expansion gives each rare glyph a direct one-token representation and shortens its decoding path, while line-to-line transition modeling injects discrete spatial displacement tokens that guide the decoder along complex reading paths without requiring precise coordinates. Experiments on two Chinese historical document OCR benchmarks show that TongGuOCR outperforms representative traditional task-specific OCR models, general-purpose multimodal large language models (MLLMs), and OCR-oriented MLLMs. On the more challenging M5HisDoc benchmark, TongGuOCR achieves 93.76 AR and reduces NED from 10.43 to 6.15 and RO-ED from 7.53 to 3.49 relative to the best competing score for each metric. An online demo is available at https://jzzh2004.github.io/TongGuOCR.

</details>


### [193] [A Hybrid Nested Harness for Decoupling Structure and Parameters in LLM-Driven Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08156)
*Víctor Gallego*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出混合嵌套搜索框架，将LLM用于结构草图的提议，数值优化器用于参数调优，显著提升进化算法效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM作为进化算法单一算子时，虽擅长结构更新，但连续参数调优效率低，浪费token且陷入试错循环。

Method: 构造双层搜索：外层LLM提议带数值空缺的结构草图，内层数值优化器（如CMA-ES、梯度法或MCMC）调优参数；内外求解器均可插拔替换，兼容任意文本优化器和零阶/一阶优化器。

Result: 在三个领域（闭式测试函数、系统研究/社会困境的代码策略、近似贝叶斯推断）中，混合优化器均优于纯LLM驱动搜索和纯数值优化基线。

Conclusion: 混合嵌套搜索有效结合LLM的结构推理与数值优化的精确性，可推广多类科学优化问题。

Abstract: In evolutionary algorithms powered by language models, the LLM acts as a single operator that simultaneously updates structural components (like control flow) and continuous parameters. While LLMs can be good at the first, they are not efficient at the second, wasting tokens taking discrete jumps inside a trial and error loop. We resolve this by formalizing a hybrid nested search, in which an outer loop has the LLM propose a structural sketch, with numeric gaps, and an inner numerical optimizer tunes the sketch. Both the outer and inner solvers are pluggable: any text-based optimizer can be combined with a zero-order optimizer (CMA-ES), gradient-based routines, or MCMC samplers. We validate our framework across three scientific domains: (i) meta-optimizers on closed-form test functions, (ii) code-based policies for systems research and social dilemmas; and (iii) approximate Bayesian inference tasks. Across all three, the hybrid optimizer is superior to both vanilla LLM-driven search and pure numerical optimization baselines. Code at: https://github.com/vicgalle/hybrid-nested-search

</details>


### [194] [ZhuLong: Execution-Grounded LLM Agent for EDA Scripting with Offline API Self-Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07925)
*Yang Liu,Shiwei Hou,Xiyuan Chen,Yu Wang,Sen Yuan,Qirui Gan,Shao You,Feifan Chen,Wencheng Li,Shuyang Hu,Yongzhou Liu,Emma Xia,Xiaojing Lu,Hao Wang,Fan Xu,Yanfeng Li*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出ZhuLong，一种面向EDA脚本编写的执行接地LLM编码代理，通过统一MCP工具集成API检索、文档检查和沙箱执行，并利用反事实实验的离线API自探索机制推断未文档化API行为，在PyAether和SKILL任务上显著优于纯LLM基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EDA脚本编写依赖工具特定的、常无文档的API，现有LLM无法有效处理这一长尾瓶颈，缺乏执行反馈和API行为推断能力。

Method: ZhuLong结合API检索、文档检查、沙箱执行和统一MCP工具，提出离线API自探索机制，通过反事实实验推断未文档化API行为，实现执行接地。

Result: 在EDA-Eval-PyAether基准（158个任务）上实现78.5% Pass@1，远超纯LLM基线（23.6%）；消融显示沙箱执行贡献41.2pp，自探索机制额外提升3.2pp并减少22.1%工具调用；交互任务上PyAether达60.0%，SKILL达50.0%。

Conclusion: ZhuLong通过执行接地和API自探索有效解决EDA脚本编写的长尾问题，证明沙箱执行是关键性能驱动，自探索机制提升准确率和效率，为EDA领域LLM代理提供可行方案。

Abstract: EDA scripting with tool-specific, often undocumented APIs remains a long-tail bottleneck that existing LLMs fail to address. This paper presents ZhuLong, an execution-grounded LLM coding agent for PyAether and SKILL that combines API retrieval, documentation inspection, and sandbox execution via unified MCP tools, augmented by an offline API self-exploration mechanism that infers undocumented API behaviors through counterfactual experimentation.
  We evaluate ZhuLong on EDA-Eval-PyAether, a benchmark of 158 real-world tasks with assertion-based execution, where the complete system achieves 78.5% Pass@1 in the commercial Empyrean Aether environment, substantially outperforming a pure LLM baseline (23.6%). Ablation studies identify sandbox execution as the dominant performance driver (41.2 pp drop when removed), with the self-exploration mechanism contributing an additional 3.2 pp accuracy gain and a 22.1% reduction in per-task tool calls. On 20 interactive tasks involving unsaved layouts and schematics, ZhuLong achieves 60.0% Pass@1 for PyAether and 50.0% for SKILL.

</details>


### [195] [Predicting blood clot growth from sparse post-onset measurements with latent neural differential equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08165)
*Lennon J. Shikhman,Ying Qian,He Li*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出基于潜在神经微分方程的框架，从稀疏测量中推断凝血模型未知参数并预测血栓进展，SNODE表现最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 凝血计算模型临床适用受限，因输入难测且患者数据稀疏。

Method: 用潜在神经微分方程（含SNODE、SNFDE等七种概率方法）结合四种生化输入和稀疏早期血栓尺寸观测，推断组织因子参数并预测后续生长。

Result: SNODE在参数推断和轨迹预测上最佳，SNFDE次之且优于非微分模型；观测增多提升精度，预测时间延长降低精度并增加不确定性。

Conclusion: 潜在神经微分方程有效结合参数推断与预测，为个性化血栓建模提供基础。

Abstract: Computational models of blood clotting improve understanding of thrombus formation, but their clinical application remains limited because many model inputs are difficult to measure and patient-specific data are often sparse. We present a computational framework based on latent neural differential equations that infers unknown model parameters from sparse measurements and forecasts thrombosis progression. We demonstrate the framework using data generated from a multiphysics blood-clotting model in which clot growth is governed by the coagulation cascade and diffusion. Four known biochemical inputs (fibrinogen and factors IX, VIII, and V), together with sparse early clot-size observations, are used to infer the tissue-factor parameter and predict subsequent clot growth. We compare seven probabilistic methods: stochastic neural ordinary differential equations (SNODE), stochastic neural functional differential equations (SNFDE), a latent neural-process baseline, a monotone probabilistic deep ensemble, empirical trajectory retrieval, PCA-ridge Gaussian posterior, and Gompertz-curve retrieval. SNODE achieved the best performance in inferring the unknown input and forecasting future clot-growth trajectories. SNFDE performed similarly and consistently outperformed the other non-differential models. Prediction accuracy improved as more observations became available, whereas longer forecasting horizons increased uncertainty and decreased accuracy. Latent neural differential equations thus effectively combine parameter inference and clot-growth forecasting from sparse measurements, providing a promising foundation for personalized thrombosis modeling.

</details>


### [196] [REIN: Bridging the Gap between Reasoning and Reliability via Reflection and Abstention Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07931)
*Zhengze Huang,Luyang Yu,Di Hong,Xinzhe Huang,Wanyu Lin,Zhixuan Chu,Zhan Qin,Tianhang Zheng*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出REIN对齐框架，通过结构化推理序列和显式弃答机制，在不增加推理开销的情况下显著减少大推理模型的推理幻觉和知识幻觉，并将幻觉代理指标降低58%~72%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大推理模型易产生幻觉，其根源分为推理幻觉（错误推理步骤导致错误结论）和知识幻觉（缺乏必要事实知识），现有方法难以同时兼顾两者且常依赖额外推理成本，因此需要一种高效统一的对齐方案。

Method: REIN分两部分：一是训练模型生成<think>→<reflection>→<answer>的结构化推理序列，强制显式自我反思；二是在奖励机制中鼓励当所有采样推理链均无正确结果时明确回答“不知道”，从而避免无依据预测。训练只需单次前向传播，无需过程监督、外部搜索或多轮批判。

Result: 在数学和常识推理基准上，REIN相对基础模型将幻觉代理指标降低58%~72%，平均覆盖率保持86%~91%，且对已尝试问题的选择性准确率提升6.6%~14.2%，同时有效减少错误但自信的回应。

Conclusion: REIN以极简训练方式和单次前向传播即可同时缓解两类幻觉，兼顾准确性与覆盖率，为安全部署大推理模型提供了高效可行方案。

Abstract: Large reasoning models (LRMs) are prone to hallucination, which undermines their reliability and poses challenges for safe deployment. Hallucinations in LRMs arise from two distinct failure sources: reasoning hallucination, where flawed inference steps propagate to an incorrect conclusion, and knowledge hallucination, where the model lacks the requisite factual knowledge to answer the query. To address reasoning hallucination, we propose REIN, an alignment framework that trains LRMs to produce a structured reasoning sequence, $\texttt{<think>} $$\rightarrow$ $\texttt{<reflection>} $$\rightarrow$ $\texttt{<answer>}$, enabling explicit self-reflection before committing to a final answer. To address knowledge hallucination, REIN introduces a reward mechanism that encourages explicit abstention (e.g., "I don't know") when none of the sampled reasoning chains yields a correct answer, allowing the model to refrain from unsupported predictions. Extensive evaluations on mathematical and commonsense reasoning benchmarks show that REIN consistently improves selective accuracy, reduces incorrect-but-self-endorsed responses, and maintains high coverage compared with competitive baselines. Notably, REIN achieves these gains within a single forward pass, without requiring process supervision, inference-time controllers, external search, or multi-round critiques. Experiments on multiple backbones show that REIN reduces the hallucination proxy by $58\sim72\%$ relative to the base models while maintaining $86\sim91\%$ average coverage, and improves selective accuracy on attempted questions by $6.6\sim14.2\%$.

</details>


### [197] [Biologically Informed Representation Learning for Robust Cross-Center Generalization of MALDI-TOF Mass Spectrometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08182)
*Alejandro L. García-Navarro,Carlos Sevilla-Salcedo,Belén Rodríguez-Sánchez,Vanessa Gómez-Verdejo*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出DALMA框架，通过联合建模采集域变异性与生物监督，学习可迁移的生物结构表征，实现跨临床中心的零样本微生物鉴定与耐药预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MALDI-TOF质谱机器学习模型因域偏移而部署受限，现有表示学习方法主要做统计域对齐，忽略了数据中天然存在的生物监督信息。

Method: 采用概率表示学习框架，域特定重建与生物引导表示学习相结合，训练时不需目标域信息，推理时无需机构特定组件。

Result: 在多中心基准（7个数据集、3个国家）上，两个留出临床中心实现最优零样本鉴定，表征可迁移至耐药预测，且潜在空间新颖性估计支持可靠选择性预测。

Conclusion: 生物信息引导的表示学习是临床微生物学中实现鲁棒、可迁移机器学习模型的有效策略。

Abstract: Machine learning models for MALDI-TOF mass spectrometry have shown considerable promise for clinical microbiology tasks such as microbial identification and antimicrobial resistance prediction. However, their deployment across institutions remains limited by domain shift, as acquisition-specific variability often leads models to capture technical artifacts rather than transferable biological information. Existing representation learning approaches primarily address this problem through statistical domain alignment while largely overlooking the biological supervision naturally available in microbiology datasets. We introduce DALMA, a probabilistic representation learning framework that jointly models acquisition-specific variability and biological supervision to learn biologically structured latent representations. By combining domain-specific reconstruction with biologically guided representation learning, DALMA learns transferable representations that generalize across heterogeneous clinical centers without requiring institution-specific components at inference, enabling zero-shot deployment on previously unseen sites. We evaluate DALMA on a multi-center benchmark comprising seven datasets from three countries. DALMA consistently achieves state-of-the-art zero-shot microbial identification across two held-out clinical centers, while the learned representations also transfer effectively to antimicrobial resistance prediction. Furthermore, latent-space novelty estimation enables reliable selective prediction under previously unseen domain shifts. These results demonstrate that biologically informed representation learning provides an effective strategy for robust and transferable ML in clinical microbiology.

</details>


### [198] [Locating Failure in Multi-Page Visually Rich Document Understanding: An Empirical Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07943)
*Lewei Xu,Yihao Ding,Zihan Xu,Daniel Yitian Su,Daochang Liu,Siwen Luo,Yifan Peng,Wei Liu*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 本文通过干预实验系统隔离了多页视觉丰富文档理解中的表示、选择和推理三类失败模式，发现视觉必要但不可替代文本提取、页面缺失限制准确率而干扰项影响小、推理器即使获得完整证据也难以跨页整合，并据此提出固定计算预算下的系统构建建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前关于多页视觉丰富文档理解系统构建方式存在相互竞争且缺乏实证检验的主张，需要通过受控实验明确各组件（视觉、文本提取、页面选择、跨页推理）的实际贡献与失效原因。

Method: 基于多页文档理解数据集，定义表示、选择、推理三种失败模式，通过每次仅干预一个环节而固定其他环节的方式，分别测试视觉与文本提取的作用、缺失页面与干扰项的影响、以及推理器在证据完整时的跨页整合能力，并进一步考察提示词对推理行为的调节效应。

Result: 视觉信息必要但无法替代文本提取；缺少页面会明显制约准确率，而额外干扰页面影响较小；即便证据全部提供，推理器仍难以整合跨页信息；提示词能显著改变推理行为，但会带来不同指标间的权衡。

Conclusion: 构建多页视觉丰富文档理解系统时应优先保证页面覆盖与文本提取质量，随后关注跨页推理能力，并谨慎使用提示词以避免顾此失彼，在固定计算预算下合理分配资源。

Abstract: Multi-page visually-rich document understanding (MP-VRDU) requires managing evidence that is sparse, spread across pages, and often exceeds a model's context window. Prior work has produced competing, largely untested claims about how these systems should be built. We attribute incorrect answers to three failure modes, representation, selection, and reasoning, and isolate each over a multi-page document understanding dataset by intervening on one while holding the others fixed. We find that vision is necessary but does not replace text extraction, that missing pages bound accuracy while distractors cost little, and that reasoners fail to integrate evidence across pages even when it is fully supplied. Prompting can shift reasoning behaviour substantially, improving some outcomes at the expense of others. We translate these findings into guidance for building such systems under a fixed compute budget.

</details>


### [199] [Control-Diverse Reinforcement Fine-Tuning: Decoupling the Shared Control Bottleneck of RL Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08224)
*Binwen Tan,Jingchao Wang,Dengzhe Hou,Lingyu Jiang,Zeyuan Wu,Yunhan Shen,Fangzhou Lin,Kazunori Yamada,Atsushi Koike*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出共享控制瓶颈（Shared Control Bottleneck）作为机制诊断指标，并据此设计控制多样化强化微调（CD-RFT）正则化，在Qwen2.5-7B和Llama-3.2-3B上实现控制解耦与多任务能力提升，且计算开销低于8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可解释性研究将RL微调成功归因于更强的电路激活，但未区分激活与控制：被激活的电路不一定控制奖励增益。需从控制角度重新审视RL后训练的内部机制。

Method: 借鉴代谢控制分析，定义后训练控制系数衡量组件对奖励增益的控制力，构建控制矩阵与激活矩阵；提出共享控制瓶颈与激活-控制差距概念，并通过一阶代理梯度正则化后训练损失，避免与flash attention不兼容的二阶微分，提出CD-RFT。

Result: CD-RFT在Qwen2.5-7B上实现最大控制解耦，数学、代码、逻辑多任务能力优于匹配GRPO；无KL变体在pass@1领先，KL惩罚变体在large-k pass@k覆盖上优于KL退化情况；控制解耦与能力提升迁移至Llama-3.2-3B。

Conclusion: 共享控制瓶颈既是机制诊断工具也是有效训练正则化器，控制解耦与能力增益具有跨模型泛化性，为RL后训练提供新的控制中心视角。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning post-training unlocks complex reasoning in LLMs. Yet benchmark scores reveal only whether a model improved, not what changed inside it, nor how it splits finite capability across tasks. A representative interpretability line attributes the success of RL fine-tuning to stronger and more diverse circuit activation. We challenge this activation-centered account by separating activation from control: an activated circuit need not control the post-training reward gain. Adapting Metabolic Control Analysis, we define the Post-training Control Coefficient to measure component control over reward gain and arrange these coefficients by task family into a control matrix, paired with an activation-magnitude matrix. We call cross-task control concentration the Shared Control Bottleneck and the difference between activation and control concentration the Activation-Control Gap. This reveals that highly shared activations can coexist with task-specific control, while a small gap indicates that control has collapsed onto a shared direction and lost task specificity. To reduce this collapse, we regularize the post-training loss with the Shared Control Bottleneck and propose Control-Diverse Reinforcement Fine-Tuning (CD-RFT). The exact regularizer gradient requires second-order automatic differentiation incompatible with flash attention, so we derive a first-order proxy with worst-case overhead below eight percent. On Qwen2.5-7B, CD-RFT achieves the largest control decoupling and improves multi-task capability over matched GRPO across mathematics, code, and logic. The no-KL variant leads on pass@1, and the KL-penalized variant leads on large-k pass@k coverage that KL otherwise degrades. Together, these results show that the Shared Control Bottleneck is both a mechanistic diagnostic and a training regularizer, and that control decoupling and capability gains transfer to Llama-3.2-3B.

</details>


### [200] [KGCache: Amortized Subgraph Retrieval for KG Reasoning with LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07954)
*Uros Stanic,Changcheng Yuan,Sabuj Laskar,Ariful Azad*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出KGCache，一种用于知识图谱问答中一跳邻居的内存缓存系统，兼容迭代遍历与单次规划两种KGQA范式，通过实体缓存和语义缓存分别实现最高1.91倍和1.06倍的加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有KGQA系统（如ToG、RoG）在回答不同问题时反复查询相同的图邻域，造成冗余KG访问，影响效率。

Method: 在KGQA引擎与KG后端之间插入内存缓存，缓存一跳邻居；支持LRU、LFU及Oracle策略，并探索基于查询语义的缓存以提升命中率。

Result: WebQSP与CWQ数据集中存在大量实体复用；实体缓存加速KG检索最高1.91倍，语义上下文缓存实现全系统1.06倍加速，单次命中延迟降低至原来的3.73倍。

Conclusion: 实体复用是KGQA工作负载的显著特征，缓存一跳邻居能有效减少KG查询；语义缓存有额外收益但需进一步验证准确性。

Abstract: Large language models can answer knowledge-intensive questions more reliably when they are grounded with knowledge graphs, but systems such as Think-on-Graph and Reasoning-on-Graph repeatedly query the same graph neighborhoods across different questions. In this work, we study this repeated retrieval in Knowledge Graph Question Answering~(KGQA) workloads and propose KGCache, an in-memory cache for one-hop knowledge graph neighborhoods. KGCache is designed to be compatible with both iterative traversal (ToG) and one shot planning (RoG) KGQA paradigms. KGCache is placed between the KGQA engine and the backend serving the KG, so repeated entity requests can be served from cache instead of issuing new KG queries. We evaluate KGCache on WebQSP and CWQ using LRU, LFU, and a trace-aware Oracle policy. Our analysis shows that both datasets contain substantial entity reuse among starting entities and entities reached during traversal. We also explore semantic caching for similar queries, which shows additional hit-rate gains on WebQSP and needs further accuracy testing on CWQ. Entity caching accelerates KG retrieval by up to $1.91\times$, while semantic-context caching achieves up to $1.06\times$ full-system speedup in the evaluated WebQSP configurations, with each hit being up to $3.73\times$ faster.

</details>


### [201] [SAGE: SLO-Aware Adaptive Retrieval for Production RAG Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08237)
*Muhammad Faizan Raza,Shuo,Yang,Satish Mahadevan Srinivasan*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: SAGE是一个学习型SLO感知自适应检索策略，动态选择每查询的检索段落数k，在不牺牲答案质量的前提下大幅提升尾延迟SLO合规性，并降低检索成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产环境中的RAG系统受严格延迟SLO和成本约束，但固定检索预算忽略查询难度差异，导致简单查询过度检索、困难查询检索不足，迫使运维在质量和SLO之间权衡。

Method: SAGE利用初始检索的轻量特征（如分数分布、排名间隙、词法信号），通过离线模仿学习从近似最优延迟-质量权衡的oracle策略中训练，推理时无需额外LLM调用，仅增加极小开销。

Result: 在Natural Questions上，5秒P95延迟SLO下，SAGE达到95%SLO合规率（静态基线k=20仅30%），P95延迟降低36%，检索成本降低51%，EM仅损失2个百分点；单策略可泛化至多个数据集和四种LLM家族，SLO改进45-52点且无质量下降。

Conclusion: SAGE证明通过轻量级自适应检索策略可同时满足严格SLO、降低成本并保持答案质量，且具备跨数据集和模型家族的强泛化能力，适合生产部署。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) systems in production operate under strict service level objectives (SLOs) on tail latency and infrastructure cost. However, standard retrieval pipelines rely on fixed retrieval budgets that ignore query difficulty, over-retrieving for easy queries and under-serving hard ones, forcing operators to trade answer quality against SLO compliance. This paper proposes SAGE, a learned SLO-aware adaptive retrieval policy that dynamically selects the number of passages k per query. SAGE uses lightweight features derived from initial retrieval (e.g., score distributions, rank gaps, lexical signals) and is trained offline via imitation learning from an oracle that approximates optimal latency-quality trade-offs. At inference, it adds no LLM calls and minimal overhead. On Natural Questions, under a 5s P95 latency SLO, SAGE achieves 95% SLO compliance versus 30% for the best static baseline (k=20), reduces P95 latency by 36% and retrieval cost by 51% with only 2 percentage points Exact Match (EM) loss. A single policy trained on Natural Questions generalizes across HotpotQA, UnSeenTimeQA, and four LLM families (Llama, Qwen, Mistral, Gemma), consistently yielding +45-52 point SLO improvements without quality degradation.

</details>


### [202] [Self-Evolving Neuro-Symbolic Skills for Tool-Augmented Spatial Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07955)
*Shi-Yu Tian,Zhuo-Xia Wang,Xuan-Yi Zhu,Zhi Zhou,Xinwei Yang,Kun-Yang Yu,Ming Yang,Yang Chen,Yu-Feng Li*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出NeSy-Spatial，一种神经符号框架，通过将工具交互和几何操作抽象为可执行原子指令，并组合成工具使用技能与几何技能，在推理时检索执行，在演化中依据成功/失败轨迹自我优化，从而在细粒度空间任务上提升推理准确性与工具利用率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型视觉语言模型在细粒度空间任务上不可靠，现有工具增强方法缺乏显式依赖约束或依赖固定流水线，泛化性差且冗余；需要一种能积累可复用经验并自适应组合的空间推理智能体。

Method: NeSy-Spatial将工具和几何操作抽象为类型化可执行原子指令，组合为工具使用技能（组织工具执行）和几何技能（结构化几何推理）两种互补技能；推理时闭环检索和执行相关技能，演化时分析缓冲的成功/失败轨迹以细化技能结构并剪枝不可靠或非活跃条目。

Result: 在三个空间推理基准上，NeSy-Spatial一致地提高了推理准确性，并实现了更精确的工具利用。

Conclusion: NeSy-Spatial通过自演化技能积累与自适应组合，有效增强了LVLM在细粒度空间推理上的可靠性，提供了一种可泛化的工具增强范式。

Abstract: Large vision-language models have achieved strong performance in multimodal reasoning, but they remain unreliable on fine-grained spatial tasks that demand both precise spatial perception and fine-grained geometric computation beyond end-to-end generation. Tool augmentation offers a natural solution, while existing methods either plan tool calls from scratch without explicit dependency constraints or rely on fixed pipelines that are redundant and generalize poorly across spatial tasks. An effective spatial reasoning agent should instead accumulate reusable experience and adaptively compose it for new problems. To this end, we propose NeSy-Spatial, a neuro-symbolic framework for self-evolving spatial skills. NeSy-Spatial abstracts tool interactions and geometric operations into typed executable atomic instructions and composes them into two complementary skill types: Tool-Use Skills for organizing tool execution and Geometry Skills for structured geometric reasoning. During inference, NeSy-Spatial retrieves and executes relevant skills in a closed-loop process. During evolution, it analyzes buffered successful and failed trajectories to refine skill structures and prune unreliable or inactive entries. Experiments on three spatial reasoning benchmarks show that NeSy-Spatial consistently improves reasoning accuracy with more precise tool utilization.

</details>


### [203] [SCOUT: Self-Checking and Recovery-Aware Tool-Thought Agents for Ultra-Long Egocentric Video Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07959)
*Keyang Zhong,Kuo Wang,Peng Liu,Quanlong Zheng,Junlin Xie,Zhijia Liang,Yanhao Zhang,Guanbin Li*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出一种具有自适应策略和不确定性优先优化的智能体框架SCOUT，用于超长第一人称视频理解，在长时程推理中平衡开发与探索，并通过改进的强化学习方法提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超长第一人称视频理解需要跨数小时或数天的稀疏证据推理，现有模型上下文有限且难以定位关键片段；CoTT智能体系统因僵化的放大策略缺乏恢复机制导致错误传播；多轮工具使用智能体的训练因稀疏结果奖励和缺乏决策轨迹监督而存在信用分配不佳问题。

Method: 提出SCOUT框架，引入自适应策略评估中间工具观察，动态权衡开发（放大）与探索（区域切换）；开发UPS-GRPO算法，聚焦高不确定性工具后状态进行探索以提高样本效率；设计回合级优势分解，结合结果奖励与工具接地时间对齐奖励改善信用分配。

Result: 在超长第一人称视频基准上达到最先进结果，并在较短时长长视频设置中保持竞争力。

Conclusion: SCOUT通过恢复感知的自适应决策和改进的强化学习训练，有效解决了超长时间跨度视频推理中的错误传播与信用分配问题，成为该任务的首选方案。

Abstract: Ultra-long egocentric video understanding requires reasoning over temporally sparse evidence distributed across hours or days, challenging current multimodal models with limited context and the grounding of key video segments. While Chain-of-Tool-Thought (CoTT) agent systems enable iterative retrieval and inspection, they suffer from error propagation due to rigid zoom-in strategies that lack recovery mechanisms. In this work, we address these challenges through SCOUT (Self-Checking Chain-Of-Tool-thought), a recovery-aware agentic framework introducing an adaptive policy that evaluates intermediate tool observations and dynamically trades off exploitation (zoom-in) and exploration (region switching), enabling robust multi-hop reasoning over extremely long horizons. However, training such multi-turn tool-using agents remains challenging, as existing RL methods rely on sparse outcome-level rewards and lack supervision over extended decision trajectories, resulting in suboptimal credit assignment for long-horizon reasoning. To address this, we develop UPS-GRPO, an uncertainty-prioritized policy optimization method that concentrates exploration on high-uncertainty post-tool states while preserving sample efficiency. We further introduce a turn-level advantage decomposition that integrates outcome rewards with tool-grounded temporal alignment rewards for improved credit assignment. Experiments show that SCOUT achieves state-of-the-art results on ultra-long egocentric benchmarks, while remaining competitive on shorter-horizon long-video settings.

</details>


### [204] [Opportunity Is Not Realizability: Selection-Valid Diagnostics for Multi-LLM Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08265)
*Ibne Farabi Shihab,Abu Sa-Adat Mohamed Moon-Im Al Ahsan,Md Najmus Swaqeeb*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出选择有效的置信区间方法，证明路由器的实际收益远小于理论上的全知神谕收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神谕路由诊断存在两个缺陷：使用同一组样本选择的最佳固定模型会使配对推断失效，且全知神谕能看到部署路由器无法观察到的结果。

Method: 区分三种估计目标（结果神谕机会、声明信号下的贝叶斯最优增益、学习路由器的留出增益），证明选择有效的置信区间，并引入信号信息三明治界和子模互补覆盖的贪心保证。

Result: 在八个检查点、四个基准上，选择有效区间显示群体神谕差距为9.7到30.7点，但最强可部署提示路由器仅恢复7.5%到14.4%，且最佳策略的同时区间下限为零。

Conclusion: 可实现的共享神谕机会很小但可验证：强路由器优于最佳固定模型，但大部分差距仍然存在。

Abstract: Oracle routing measures how much a pool of language models could gain from per-query selection, but the diagnostic has two flaws: testing against a best fixed model selected on the same examples invalidates paired inference, and a full-information oracle sees outcomes no deployable router observes. We separate three estimands (outcome-oracle opportunity, the Bayes-optimal gain from a declared pre-answer signal, and the held-out gain of a learned router) and prove selection-valid confidence intervals that survive choosing the best fixed model or the best member of a router family, a signal-information sandwich, and a $(1-1/e)$ greedy guarantee for building compact pools from submodular complementary coverage. On eight checkpoints from six families over four benchmarks, selection-valid intervals certify a population oracle gap of $9.7$--$30.7$ points on every task, yet the strongest deployable prompt router recovers only $7.5$--$14.4\%$ of it, and the simultaneous interval for the best of eleven tested policies has lower limit zero throughout. The realizable share of oracle opportunity is small and certifiable: strong routers beat the best fixed model, and most of the gap remains.

</details>


### [205] [VDGR-RAG: Vectors, Directories, Graphs, and Reflection Are All You Need for Unified Reasoning over Hierarchical Enterprise Knowledge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07994)
*Wenqi Chen,Haofei Yang,Rui Yang,Fangming Li*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出VDGR-RAG，一个集成了向量检索、目录驱动推理、图遍历和迭代反思的统一Agentic GraphRAG系统，用于企业知识问答，显著优于现有RAG基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG方法缺乏对多种检索优势的整合，导致领域路由不准确、层次文档结构利用不足以及企业知识推理能力受限。

Method: 构建层次异构知识图（H²KG）保留目录结构和语义关系，并设计四种原子工具：目录增强路由、多路由检索（向量+TOC+图）、目录回溯修正偏差、动态反思规划下一阶段检索。

Result: 在四个无线领域的企业产品文档上，所提方法在知识检索召回率和问答准确率上均显著超过多种RAG基线。

Conclusion: VDGR-RAG通过统一融合多样检索机制，有效提升企业复杂文档的问答性能，验证了Agentic GraphRAG的实用价值。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) is essential for enterprise knowledge question answering (QA), particularly in domains with complex product documentation like telecommunications. However, existing RAG approaches largely overlook the holistic integration of diverse retrieval strengths, leading to inaccurate domain routing, poor utilization of hierarchical document structures, and consequently limited reasoning capabilities over enterprise knowledge. To address these limitations, we present VDGR-RAG, which integrates vector retrieval, directory-driven reasoning, graph traversal, and iterative reflection in a unified framework for accurate enterprise knowledge QA. Specifically, VDGR-RAG is an agentic GraphRAG system that first constructs a Hierarchical Heterogeneous Knowledge Graph ($\text{H}^2$KG) from document chunks to preserve both hierarchical directory structures and semantic relationships, and then employs a set of atomic tools for knowledge retrieval that can be freely composed to navigate the $\text{H}^2$KG: (1) a directory-enhanced routing tool that uses table-of-contents (TOC) structures to route user queries to appropriate domain-specific $\text{H}^2$KGs; (2) a multi-route retrieval tool that combines vector search, TOC-based agentic search, and graph search for comprehensive knowledge retrieval; (3) a directory backtracking tool that corrects knowledge localization biases; and (4) a dynamic reflection tool that iteratively plans the next retrieval phase. We conduct extensive experiments on our enterprise product documents across four wireless domains (e.g., energy saving and fault management). Experimental results demonstrate that our method significantly outperforms a variety of RAG baselines in terms of both knowledge retrieval recall and QA accuracy.

</details>


### [206] [Stateful CARS: Exact Cross-History Reuse for Policy-Constrained LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08282)
*Ibne Farabi Shihab,Md Najmus Swaqeeb,Abu Sa-Adat Mohamed Moon-Im Al Ahsan*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出一种状态化CARS方法，通过冻结状态-延续模式并在不同历史间复用无效性证书，实现从带硬状态验证器的模型分布中精确采样，并给出理论保证与实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工具使用语言模型智能体面临随观察和先前动作变化的约束，需要从满足硬状态验证器的分布中精确采样，同时高效复用无效性证书。

Method: 在每个尝试内冻结一组健全的状态-延续模式，移除所有包含匹配抽象状态的已认证延续的轨迹，并利用残差Doob变换从提议分布中精确采样；提出可检查的未来有效性互模拟条件，并证明模式健全性、自适应精确性、独立同分布输出、几乎必然终止、单调接受和压缩不变性。

Result: 在可枚举工作流上，所提方法在有效性概率为6×10^-8时与真实条件分布匹配到10^-16，而状态感知局部解码偏差可达0.97；但与官方CARS相比，采样步骤比率为0.942（置信区间[0.934,0.951]），Qwen对比无显著差异（比率0.99，CI [0.90,1.08]），跨历史迁移仅在内部匹配键消融中有效（1.27倍）。

Conclusion: 证据支持精确的模式诱导条件化，但并未显示相对CARS的系统性优势；研究还指出历史依赖语言模型的完整历史乘积状态数可能指数增长，因此不做通用有限trie可扩展性声明。

Abstract: Tool-using language-model agents face constraints whose meaning changes with observations and prior actions. We study exact sampling from the model distribution conditioned on a hard stateful validator while reusing invalidity certificates across histories. Stateful CARS freezes a bank of sound state--continuation schemas within each attempt and removes every trajectory containing a certified continuation at a matching abstract state. An exact residual Doob transform samples from the resulting proposal. We give a checkable future-validity bisimulation condition, prove schema soundness, adaptive exactness, i.i.d.\ outputs, almost-sure termination, monotone acceptance, and compression invariance, and characterize computation by the number of reachable full-history product states. This number can be exponential for a history-dependent language model; the evaluated method therefore makes no generic finite-trie scalability claim. On enumerable workflows, its analytic law matches the valid conditional to $10^{-16}$ at validity probability $6\times10^{-8}$, whereas state-aware local decoding can be $0.97$ away. A matched comparison is negative: observation-keyed official CARS is cheaper in sampler steps (root/Stateful ratio $0.942$ $[0.934,0.951]$), and the Qwen comparison is null ($0.99$ $[0.90,1.08]$). Cross-history transfer helps only in an internal matched-key ablation ($1.27\times$). Thus the evidence supports exact schema-induced conditioning, not a systems advantage over CARS.

</details>


### [207] [Thought-Level Beam Search for Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08020)
*Lijie Yang,Hongyin Luo,Tri Dao,Ravi Netravali*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出Gambit算法，通过思想级波束搜索在固定硬件预算下动态分配计算资源给最有希望的推理轨迹，相比现有方法在准确率、吞吐量和token效率上全面胜出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型推理模型的测试时计算扩展是性能关键，但现有方法效率极低：并行采样独立处理轨迹导致内存瓶颈，剪枝法又使硬件利用率不足且难以主动改变输出分布，因此核心问题从计算量转向计算分配位置。

Method: Gambit执行思想级波束搜索，周期性地剪除不具前景的轨迹，并立即从高质量前缀分支扩展；利用轻量级评分器探测隐藏状态来评估轨迹质量，从而持续维持高硬件利用率并集中计算资源到最有希望的推理路径。

Result: 在相同硬件约束下，相比剪枝基线在HMMT-24上绝对准确率提升+6.7%，在AIME-25上提升+3.3%；轨迹完成吞吐量提高2倍以上；相比标准并行采样减少最多68.5%的token消耗。

Conclusion: Gambit通过动态计算分配策略严格优于现有基线，证明在受限硬件下主动集中计算到最有希望的推理轨迹是提升LRM性能的关键方向。

Abstract: Test-time compute scaling is a primary driver of performance in large reasoning models (LRMs), but extreme inefficiency bounds current approaches, shifting the critical question from \emph{how much} compute to spend, to \emph{where} to allocate it. We formalize test-time reasoning as a constrained compute allocation problem over partial trajectories. Under a fixed hardware budget, existing paradigms fail to actively allocate the compute to the most promising partial progress: traditional parallel sampling treats traces independently and induces severe memory bottlenecks, while subtractive pruning starves hardware and fails to actively and sufficiently shift the output distribution. To overcome this dichotomy, we introduce Gambit, an inference algorithm that executes \emph{thought-level beam search}. By periodically pruning unpromising trajectories and immediately branching from high-quality prefixes, Gambit dynamically concentrates compute onto the most promising reasoning traces via a light-weight scorer probing hidden states while maintaining continuous high hardware utilization. Extensive evaluations across multiple models and benchmarks demonstrate that Gambit strictly dominates existing baselines. Under identical hardware constraints, our method yields up to a +6.7\% absolute accuracy gain on HMMT-24 and +3.3\% on AIME-25 over pruning baselines, delivers $>2\times$ higher throughput on trace completion, and reduces total token consumption by up to 68.5\% relative to standard parallel sampling.

</details>


### [208] [SkillSmith: Enhancing Locally Deployed Agents via Automatic Skill Construction and Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08037)
*Xinle Jiang,Remy Xie,Ming Tang*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出SkillSmith框架，通过云-端协作自动化构建和演化技能知识，使本地小模型在任务效果上达到云端大模型水平，同时降低推理成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有本地智能体因模型规模受限，缺乏环境规则和操作流程等环境知识，任务效果远逊于云端智能体，且手动专家编写知识不可行。

Method: SkillSmith采用云-端协作：云端智能体探索任务并自动生成技能（Skill）作为上下文高效的知识载体，本地智能体执行时反馈错误，技能据此迭代演化，全程无需专家编写，且冻结本地模型参数。

Result: 在AppWorld和WorkBench上，使用Qwen3.6-27B的本地智能体达到与云端大模型相当的效果，超越最强非参数基线，并将AppWorld-Normal平均行动数从36.1降至9.9，且技能可迁移至其他SLM。

Conclusion: 非参数化技能知识供给可有效弥补本地小模型的知识缺口，实现低成本高性能的本地智能体部署，并具备良好泛化性。

Abstract: LLM-based agent frameworks now act as personal assistants for multi-step tasks. Existing agent frameworks such as OpenClaw commonly follow the Cloud Agent depolyment mode using closed-source cloud LLMs as backbone model, which may expose private user information and incur repeated LLM-calling costs. Local Agents address these deployment concerns by depolying frontier open-source SLMs on user-controlled devices, but their task effectiveness still lags far behind Cloud Agents. Through diagnostic analysis, we reveal that the limited effectiveness of Local Agents with frontier SLM backbones mainly comes from missing environment knowledge caused by limited backbone model scale including environment rules and operation procedures. To supply such knowledge non-parametrically, context-efficiently, and without expert authoring, we present SkillSmith, a Cloud--Local Agent collaboration framework that uses Skill as a context-efficient knowledge carrier, automatic constructs Skill from Cloud Agent task exploration and evolves Skill using Local Agent execution feedback to enhance a frozen Local Agent. Experiments on daily agent task datasets AppWorld and WorkBench show that the automatically generated Skill enables the Local Agent with Qwen3.6-27B(SLM) to achieve task effectiveness comparable to Cloud Agents with frontier LLMs, outperform the strongest non-parametric baselines, reduce average actions per task from 36.1 to 9.9 on AppWorld-Normal, and generalize to other SLM backbone models without rerunning Skill construction.

</details>


### [209] [JustLLMGRPO: Radiographic Control for Chest X-Ray Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08046)
*Pengxiang Cai,Xiaohan Li,Anglin Liu,Qingyuan Zeng,Zexun Li,Jintai Chen*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 通过仅优化提示文本策略而非生成器，JustLLMGRPO在保持源提示语义对齐的同时，将胸片生成的RadDINO-FID从54.225降至26.780，提升50.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本条件胸片生成研究集中于更新图像生成器，忽略了提示优化这一维度；实验发现仅用LLM改写提示即可大幅改善生成质量，但会损害语义对齐，因此需要一种同时兼顾生成质量和提示对齐的方法。

Method: 冻结CXR适配的Sana生成器，仅使用GRPO优化LLM的提示改写策略，以组内相对奖励（结合RadDINO-FID和BioViL-T对齐）引导提示生成，保留可渲染的影像发现，抑制不可渲染的报告内容。

Result: 在CheXGenBench上，JustLLMGRPO将RadDINO-FID降至26.780（较直接提示改进50.6%），BioViL-T对齐保持在0.696（基线为0.695），同时实现最优分布覆盖率和下游分类效用。

Conclusion: 提示文本的表达方式对适配生成器有巨大影响，通过强化学习优化提示策略可获得显著性能提升，为多模态生成提供新维度。

Abstract: Text-conditioned chest X-ray generation aims to synthesize realistic radiographs that faithfully depict specified findings. Existing work has primarily improved quality by updating image generators, implicitly treating prompts as fixed after CXR-domain adaptation. We show that this generator-centric view leaves a substantial optimization dimension underexplored. With a CXR-adapted Sana generator frozen, one-pass reformulation by an unmodified LLM reduces RadDINO-FID from 54.225 to 27.572. Prompt analysis shows that the LLM suppresses temporal comparisons, uncertainty, and other non-renderable report content while emphasizing visible radiographic findings. However, unconstrained reformulation reduces BioViL-T alignment with source prompts from 0.695 to 0.609. We therefore introduce JustLLMGRPO, which applies standard Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) only to the LLM prompt policy while keeping Sana frozen. Group-relative radiology-aware image feedback retains visual focus while preserving source-prompt alignment. On CheXGenBench, JustLLMGRPO reduces RadDINO-FID to 26.780, a 50.6% improvement over direct prompting, while maintaining alignment (0.696 versus 0.695). It also achieves state-of-the-art distribution coverage and downstream classification utility. These results show that substantial performance can remain latent in how radiographic information is expressed to an adapted generator. Code is publicly available at https://github.com/pxcai/JustLLMGRPO.

</details>


### [210] [SodaMem: Evidence-Grounded Temporal Graph Memory for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08055)
*Fengrong Wan,Chengcan Wu,Ningtao Lyu*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: SodaMem是一种基于证据的时间图记忆系统，用于长期LLM代理，通过提取带来源的事实事件、维护时间信息和关系边，在LongMemEval-S上以低成本达到92.8%的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM代理的平面RAG或Markdown日志虽擅长检索特定事实，但无法有效处理信息的时效性、来源追踪和有序时间推理，导致长期对话中记忆过时或混淆。

Method: SodaMem采用三阶段方法：(1)提取带强制来源跨度的事实事件；(2)持久化提及时间、发生时间和有效性，并用SUPERSEDES/CONTRADICTS/UPDATES边连接，配合混合词汇-稠密索引；(3)通过规划器-阅读器循环先收集可引用证据再生成最终回答。

Result: 在LongMemEval-S上达到92.8%准确率（500题中464题正确，N=3最佳），平均每题成本0.00161美元（约18.3k tokens），中位数0.00111美元（约14.6k tokens）。成本-准确率图上，SodaMem以Flash级别成本位于准确率前沿，优于多个高成本低准确率系统。

Conclusion: SodaMem证明了证据支撑的时间图记忆能在长期对话场景中高效平衡准确性与成本，但结果存在自评偏差（同一模型作为阅读器和评判者），且跨系统比较基于估算而非统一测试环境。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents that assist users over weeks of conversation must remember what is currently true, not merely what was once said. Flat RAG diaries and Markdown logs optimize needle retrieval but under-serve currency, provenance, and ordered temporal reasoning (Maharana et al. 2024; Wu et al. 2024; Packer et al. 2023; Chhikara et al. 2025). We present SodaMem, an evidence-grounded temporal graph memory that (i) extracts typed FactEvents with mandatory provenance spans, (ii) persists mention time, occurrence time, and validity with SUPERSEDES/CONTRADICTS/UPDATES edges under hybrid lexical-dense indexing, and (iii) answers via a planner-reader loop that gathers citable evidence before composing a final response. On LongMemEval-S, our store-of-record configuration reaches 92.8% accuracy (464/500; best of N=3) at mean $0.00161/question (approximately 18.3k tokens; median $0.00111 / approximately 14.6k) with deepseek-v4-flash. We compile public systems with estimable API cost into a cost table and cost-accuracy map; under these estimates SodaMem sits near the accuracy frontier at Flash-tier spend and strictly dominates several higher-cost, lower-accuracy points. Accuracy uses the same Flash model as reader and judge (self-grading); costs exclude ingest/judge and cross-system comparisons are compiled estimates rather than a single-harness bake-off.Our code is available at https://github.com/SodaMem/SodaMem

</details>


### [211] [CORDA: A Benchmark for Hierarchical Harm-Centric Moral Reasoning in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08061)
*Siddarth Singh,Victoria Williams,Simon Rosen,Ebenezer Gelo,Helen Sarah Robertson,Ibrahim Suder,Benjamin Rosman,Geraud Nangue Tasse,Steven James*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 本文提出CORDA基准，用于评估大语言模型在道德原则冲突时进行分层、以伤害为中心的道德推理能力，发现模型普遍存在强烈的道义论默认倾向，且难以灵活遵循指定的优先级排序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM道德评估多关注答案是否可接受或是否符合人类偏好，未测试模型在无无成本选项时如何在竞争性原则间进行优先级排序，缺乏对冲突情境下道德可控性的评估。

Method: 构建CORDA基准，基于道德链形式化，包含90个道德困境（电车难题、医疗权衡、资源分配、人-动物-机器人冲突），覆盖四种有序伦理框架（效用、效用+代理伤害、双过程、双过程+代理伤害），测试模型在道德优先级变化时能否调整决策。

Result: 在10个指令微调模型中，9个优先避免直接个人伤害而非减少总体伤害；模型在分类性伤害规避规则上表现更可靠，但在结果导向的比较推理上较弱；所有模型对显式链条件有响应，但多个模型未能一致遵循指定优先级（如人类>动物>机器人）。

Conclusion: 道德可靠性不仅需要默认的克制，更需要在冲突情境下的可控性；CORDA填补了LLM道德评估的空白，揭示了模型在应用情境化道德优先级方面的不足。

Abstract: The key question in moral judgement is not simply whether someone chooses the "right" answer, but how they decide what matters most when moral principles conflict. Current evaluations of large language models (LLMs) remain limited: most test whether models give morally acceptable answers, match human preferences, or avoid obvious violations, rather than whether they can prioritise between competing principles when no option is morally cost-free. We introduce CORDA (Conditioned Ordering and Ranked Directive Adherence), a benchmark for evaluating hierarchical, harm-centred moral reasoning in LLMs. Building on the morality chains formalism, CORDA tests 90 moral dilemmas involving trolley-style cases, medical trade-offs, resource allocation, and human-animal-robot conflicts across four ordered ethical frameworks: Utility, Utility + Agent Harm, Dual-Process, and Dual-Process + Agent Harm. Together, these frameworks test whether models can adapt their decisions when moral priorities change. Across ten instruction-tuned models from seven providers, we find a strong deontological default, with 9 of 10 prioritising avoidance of direct personal harm over reducing overall harm. Models also perform more reliably on categorical harm-avoidance rules, such as avoiding killing, than on outcome-based comparisons, such as minimising total harm, suggesting that they recognise moral red lines more easily than they reason through competing harms. Although all models respond to explicit chain conditioning, several fail to consistently follow specified priority orderings, such as humans over animals and animals over robots. CORDA addresses a central gap in LLM moral evaluation by testing whether models can move beyond default harm-avoidant responses and apply context-specified moral priorities. Moral reliability requires more than default restraint; it requires controllability under conflict.

</details>


### [212] [Think Deep, Speak Once: Relit, A Recursive Latent Implicit Transformer Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08113)
*Abhishek Panwar,Maheep Singh,Saksham Bansal*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出ReLIT混合框架，将轻量递归模块嵌入冻结的LLM骨干，在连续隐状态中实现深度推理，无需生成显式思维链，在逻辑推理基准上以极少参数匹配或超越更大模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CoT提示虽提升推理能力但产生高计算开销，而现有隐式推理方法（如TRMs）在自然语言语义保持上不足，需在符号推理与语义连贯之间取得平衡。

Method: ReLIT在冻结的TinyLlama-1.1B骨干上附加可训练的递归块，通过梯度隔离的循环迭代优化隐状态z，将深层递归推理嵌入基础模型的语义表征中，避免显式token生成。

Result: 在GLoRE基准（ProofWriter、RuleTaker等）上实现了高参数效率，匹配甚至超越明显更大的模型，且仅需极少量监督。

Conclusion: 推理能力可通过递归深度而非参数宽度扩展，为语义化的隐式推理提供了可行框架。

Abstract: Chain-of-Thought (CoT) prompting has become the dominant paradigm for eliciting reasoning in Large Language Models (LLMs), yet it creates substantial computational overhead by forcing models to externalize intermediate reasoning steps as discrete tokens. Recent latent reasoning approaches attempt to internalize this process within continuous hidden states. One of the latest advancements in the field of latent reasoning, Tiny Recursive Models (TRMs) excel at symbolic reasoning but struggle to preserve semantic coherence in natural language settings. To bridge this gap, we introduce ReLIT (Recursive Latent Implicit Transformer), a hybrid framework that grounds deep recursive reasoning within the rich semantic representations of a foundational model. ReLIT augments a frozen LLM backbone (TinyLlama-1.1B) with a lightweight, trainable recursive block that iteratively refines its latent thinking (z) before committing to a final output, structurally solving linguistic intuition from algorithmic processing and enabling "deep thinking" via gradient-isolated recurrent loops without the latency of explicit token generation. Empirically, ReLIT achieves high parameter efficiency on the GLoRE logical reasoning benchmark, matching or outperforming significantly larger models on challenging tasks such as ProofWriter and RuleTaker despite minimal supervision. These results demonstrate that reasoning capability can be scaled efficiently through recurrent depth rather than parameter width, offering a principled framework for semantically grounded implicit reasoning.

</details>


### [213] [MGMCL: Multi-Granularity Manifold Contrastive Learning With Neural ODEs for Cross-Subject EEG Emotion Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08440)
*Xiang Xie*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出MGMCL框架，在SPD黎曼流形上以多粒度对比学习和神经ODE建模连续情绪，用Gromov-Wasserstein对齐跨被试，在三个数据集上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有跨被试EEG情绪识别因个体差异大且离散化建模忽略情绪连续性，欧氏空间方法仅对齐边缘分布，无法保持情绪语义结构。

Method: 将情绪识别重构为SPD流形上的连续表示学习，引入实例/情绪/轨迹三层粒度对比学习保留语义顺序，用流形神经ODE建模动态，采用Gromov-Wasserstein流形对齐提升跨被试泛化，弱监督学习实现从离散标签预测连续VAD值。

Result: SEED准确率91.23%，SEED-IV 73.82%，DEAP 76.38%，分别较之前最优提升1.89%、1.66%、1.28%。

Conclusion: 在黎曼流形上建模连续情绪并保留语义结构可有效提升跨被试EEG情绪识别性能。

Abstract: Cross-subject electroencephalogram (EEG)-based emotion recognition remains challenging due to substantial inter-individual variability and discrete formulation that overlooks affective continuity. Existing methods operate in Euclidean space and focus on marginal distribution alignment, failing to preserve the semantic structure of emotions across subjects. This article proposes MGMCL, reconceptualizing emotion recognition as learning continuous representations on symmetric positive definite (SPD) Riemannian manifolds. The frame?work introduces multi-granularity manifold contrastive learning at instance, emotion, and trajectory levels while preserving semantic ordering. Neural ordinary differential equations on manifolds model continuous emotion dynamics. Cross-subject generalization employs Gromov-Wasserstein manifold alignment. Weakly-supervised learning enables continuous valence-arousal-dominance prediction from discrete labels. Extensive experiments on three public datasets demonstrate state-of-the-art performance: 91.23% accuracy on SEED, 73.82% on SEED-IV, and 76.38% on DEAP, achieving consistent improvements of 1.89%, 1.66%, and 1.28% over previous best methods, respectively.

</details>


### [214] [Neurosymbolic Discovery of Algebraic Graph Constructions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08118)
*David Seka,Stefan Szeider*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出了一种神经符号方法，利用通用大语言模型与SageMath交互，自动为给定的图发现简短的代数构造描述（如Cayley图或字典积），无需微调或专门训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图搜索方法（如SAT求解器）只返回原始数据（邻接矩阵或字符串编码），虽能证明图存在，但无法揭示其结构属性。希望自动从原始数据中发现简洁的代数描述，以增强对图结构的理解。

Method: 构建一个智能体，基于通用LLM（无微调），通过MCP服务器与SageMath交互。智能体循环执行：分析目标图→提出候选构造→用SageMath测试→根据单个精确同构测试结果修订，直到匹配目标。

Result: 在100个高度对称的图（最多25个顶点的双轨道图）基准上，智能体为所有图找到了经过验证的代数构造；而强模板枚举基线仅达约20%，目录查找无法识别任何图。在对称性降低时，构造质量下降。具体应用：为最小的16顶点Bernhart-Kainen可分散性猜想反例找到了显式代数构造。

Conclusion: 神经符号方法能有效为高度对称图自动发现简短代数描述，优于传统基线，但依赖对称性；展示了在组合猜想反例分析中的实用价值。

Abstract: There are several methods for searching for graphs with prescribed properties, such as SAT solvers and specialized generators. These methods return the result as raw data: an adjacency matrix or a string encoding. The raw data certifies that the graph exists, but it does not reveal any structural properties of the graph. We ask whether one can automatically discover a short algebraic description if only this raw data is provided. We look for a description such as a Cayley graph $\mathrm{Cay}(Γ, S)$ or a lexicographic product $C_5[K_3]$.
  We address this question with a neurosymbolic approach. We propose an agent that runs on a general-purpose large language model with no fine-tuning or per-target training. The model interleaves reasoning with calls to the computer algebra system SageMath: it analyzes the target graph, proposes and tests candidate constructions, and revises them until the output matches the target. The agent communicates with SageMath through a Model Context Protocol (MCP) server, which we release as a general-purpose bridge. Whether a construction matches the target is checked by a single exact isomorphism test, and therefore rests on the symbolic side and not on the model. We test the approach on a benchmark of 100 highly symmetric graphs, namely two-orbit graphs on up to 25 vertices; the benchmark was fixed in advance. Our agent could find verified algebraic constructions for all of them, without falling back to raw encodings. A strong template-enumeration baseline reaches only about $20\%$, and a catalog lookup could not identify any of these graphs. However, construction quality declines when symmetry is removed.
  As a concrete application, we identify the smallest known counterexample to the Bernhart-Kainen dispersability conjecture, a $16$-vertex graph that enumeration found as raw data. For this graph, our agent found an explicit algebraic construction.

</details>


### [215] [Task-to-Model Optimization for Enterprise LLM Coding Assistants: A Data-Driven Framework for Cost-Optimal Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08528)
*Srinivasan Manoharan,Junhua Zhao,Fangbo Tu,Haifeng Wu,Jian Wan,Maliah Rajan M,Ashwin Hegde,Mithun Sasidharan,Kalyan Chakravarthi Podamekala*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出任务到模型优化（T2MO）框架，通过九阶段流水线优化企业AI编码助手的模型选择，以每次任务完成成本为核心目标，而非单纯最小化token成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业AI编码助手推理开销巨大，朴素token成本最小化在考虑重试、升级和开发者等待时间后，往往无法降低端到端成本，需要更全面的成本优化方法。

Method: T2MO框架包括九阶段：遥测采集、分类发现、难度分级、基准构建、候选评估、最优组合推导、预测与版本规划、分阶段路由部署、持续治理；采用每完成任务成本目标，显式计及失败升级成本，并推导路由边界和两级决策层次（类别与难度档）。

Result: 证明了在升级机制下，期望完成成本目标弱支配token成本最小化；通过流量加权节约瀑布图聚合各单元替代机会，按实际美元影响排序候选模型。

Conclusion: 该框架支持开发者指南、支出预测和从静态策略到影子模式、验证级联、最终智能路由器的分阶段过渡，适合生产部署和未来实证研究。

Abstract: Enterprise AI coding assistants incur substantial inference spend, and naive token-cost minimization often fails to reduce end-to-end cost once retries, escalations, and developer wait time are included. We present Task-to-Model Optimization (T2MO), a data-driven methodology for optimizing model selection in production coding workflows. We treat each developer session as a task that can be discovered, classified, graded for difficulty, benchmarked in a production-like harness, and routed to the cheapest model able to complete it within quality and latency constraints. The framework is a nine-stage pipeline spanning telemetry instrumentation, taxonomy discovery, difficulty grading, benchmark construction, candidate evaluation, optimal mix derivation, forecasting and version planning, staged routing deployment, and continuous governance. Unlike token-centric routing rules, our objective is cost per completed task, with failure escalation priced in explicitly. We show that this expected-completion-cost objective weakly dominates token-cost minimization under escalation, and we derive the routing boundary, the minimum pass rate a cheaper model must reach on a given cell to be worth deploying. Decisions are organized as a two-level hierarchy of task category difficulty tier, and per-cell displacement opportunities are aggregated into a traffic-weighted savings waterfall that ranks replacement candidates by realized dollar impact. The framework supports developer guidance, spend forecasting, and a staged transition from static policies to shadow-mode classifiers, verified cascades, and ultimately an intelligent router. We describe the methodology, optimization objective, evaluation protocol, and governance loop in a form suitable for production deployment and future empirical study.

</details>


### [216] [Long SKILL Compliance as Logical Reasoning: Closure-Grounded Detection with Scaling-Guided On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08146)
*Shuaitao Zhao,Feng Ni,Lichao Ma,Jiaye Lin,Fei Han,Yang Wei,Lu Pan*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出SkillCDG图框架，用双层约束依赖图建模长SKILL文档，通过两级检索和依赖闭包实现合规检测与溯源，在保证F1提升12.8个百分点的同时减少最多64.3%的token消耗，并发现复杂度可量化模型性能提升潜力，指导小模型蒸馏训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业长SKILL文档使大模型推理成本高、小模型准确性不足，现有方法难以平衡效率与精度。

Method: 构建双层约束依赖图（上层场景路由、下层原子约束依赖），推理时采用两级检索加依赖闭包完成合规判断与追溯，并基于复杂度-规模缩放规律进行自适应样本选择和同策略蒸馏。

Result: 在企业数据集和公开基准变体上，F1最高提升12.8个百分点，token消耗最多降低64.3%；四个检查点实验验证了复杂度差异化缩放趋势。

Conclusion: 图结构复杂度可有效量化实例难度与模型改进潜力，指导小模型高效增强，实现精度与成本的协同优化。

Abstract: The increasing complexity of enterprise business scenarios has promoted the widespread adoption of long SKILL documents in agent systems, posing new challenges for compliance detection: large models incur substantial inference costs, while small models may fail to maintain detection accuracy. To address this gap, we propose SkillCDG, a graph-based framework for long SKILL compliance detection. SkillCDG represents complex business policies as a two-layer constraint dependency graph, where the upper layer indexes SKILL descriptions for scenario routing and the lower layer captures dependencies among atomic constraints within each SKILL. During inference, two-level retrieval followed by dependency closure supports compliance judgment and source traceability. We comprehensively evaluate the framework on three enterprise datasets and two controlled public benchmark variants. Experimental results demonstrate that SkillCDG outperforms baseline methods by up to 12.8 percentage points in detection F1 score, while reducing token consumption by a maximum 64.3\%. Moreover, we further investigate the inherent relationships among policy-graph complexity, model scale, and detection performance. Comparative experiments conducted on four checkpoints from a single model family validate a concise and effective scaling trend: end-to-end detection correctness exhibits a complexity-differentiated scaling pattern, and the complexity metric derived from the constraint dependency graph can effectively quantify instance difficulty and the performance improvement potential of models. Leveraging this insightful scaling trend, we conduct adaptive training sample selection and adopt on-policy distillation to efficiently enhance the compliance detection capability of small-scale models.

</details>


### [217] [Neural Message Passing on Structural Interaction Graphs for Fully-Inductive Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08567)
*Omer Yom Tov,Avigdor Gal*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: SIGIL通过将任意属性图映射到固定维度的统一表示空间，解决了图基础模型中特征空间异构性的迁移挑战，并实现了对知识图谱推理模型的严格泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图基础模型面临输入异构性（特征维度、语义、结构差异），限制了GNN对新图及未见特征空间的泛化能力，亟需统一映射框架。

Method: 将图提升为结构交互图（节点为特征维度，边编码多阶连接上的特征对齐），用关系消息传递网络嵌入各特征维度，得到任意维特征的可迁移表示，且天然具备置换等变性。

Result: 单一模型经单图预训练即可实现强全归纳链接预测，并可作为现有知识图谱基础模型的实现工具，统一多种图基础模型设计范式。

Conclusion: SIGIL为图基础模型提供通用统一框架，显著提升跨图迁移能力并桥接图与知识图谱推理领域。

Abstract: A central obstacle in building graph foundation models is the input heterogeneity in terms of feature space dimensionality, semantics, and structure. Such heterogeneity limits the capability of graph neural networks to generalize to new graphs with unseen feature spaces. We address the transferability challenge with SIGIL, a framework that maps any attributed graph to a unified representation space of fixed dimension. Given a graph, SIGIL lifts it to a structural interaction graph, where nodes are the input feature dimensions and weighted, typed edges encode feature alignment across multiple orders of the graph's connectivity. A relational message-passing network embeds each feature dimension into a shared space, transforming the original node features, of arbitrary dimensionality, into representations transferable to any downstream graph. By construction, SIGIL is equivariant to permutations of nodes, feature dimensions, and labels. Additionally, when the input features are one-hot indicators of discrete relations, SIGIL recovers and strictly generalizes existing foundation models for knowledge graph reasoning. A single SIGIL model, pretrained on one graph, delivers strong fully-inductive link prediction. Also, SIGIL can be used to implement existing knowledge graph foundation models. As such, SIGIL unifies several existing regimes in graph foundation model design under a single framework

</details>


### [218] [Matching Supervision to the Student's Learning Capacity: A Unified Framework for On-Policy Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08176)
*Yongkang Yang,Zhezheng Hao,Hong Zhang,Yi Liu,Xiankun Lin,Wence Ji,Fanjunduo Wei,Jiarui Yu,Qiang Lin,Xiaoyun Liang,Hande Dong*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出统一在线策略自蒸馏（USD）方法，将token加权与特权信息调整联合优化，通过拉格朗日对偶变量统一控制，显著提升LLM推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有两线工作分别优化token选择或特权信息量，但忽略二者耦合关系，导致次优解。

Method: 构建以聚合师生散度为目标、学习难度为预算的优化框架，设计轻量在线算法求解其拉格朗日对偶，用单一对偶变量同时决定token阈值与特权信息调整方向。

Result: 在多种模型规模和推理基准上，USD一致优于OPSD及token侧、PI侧基线。

Conclusion: 统一建模两变量可有效匹配学生动态容量，为自蒸馏提供更优解。代码已开源。

Abstract: On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) improves the reasoning abilities of LLMs by internalizing privileged context into model parameters through self-distillation.
  Two recent research lines promote vanilla OPSD by choosing which tokens to learn from and by controlling how much privileged information the teacher receives, respectively.
  However, we show that each line optimizes one variable while holding the other fixed, which leads to a suboptimal solution.
  We argue that the two variables are coupled through the student's learning capacity: the privileged information sets the per-token divergence the teacher prescribes, while token weighting selects which of these the student must absorb.
  We formalize the two lines of work into a unified optimization framework, which maximizes the aggregate teacher--student divergence, subject to a budget on the aggregate learning difficulty the student can absorb.
  Under this modelling, we propose Unified On-Policy Self-Distillation (USD), a lightweight online algorithm to solve the Lagrangian.
  USD reveals that a single dual variable governs both decisions: at one price for learning difficulty, it simultaneously sets the token-selection threshold and the direction of privileged-information adjustment, keeping supervision matched to the student's evolving capacity.
  Through extensive experiments, USD consistently demonstrates superior performance over OPSD and token- and PI-side baselines across various model scales on various reasoning benchmarks. Code is available at https://github.com/lauvlalala/USD.

</details>


### [219] [Quantization Degradation in Large Language Models: A Signal-Noise Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08188)
*Chenxi Zhou,Pengfei Cao,Jinyu Ye,Bohan Yu,Haida Yu,Jiang Li,Jun Zhao,Kang Liu*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了LLM权重量化的性能退化机制，发现退化程度不仅取决于比特宽度，还受量化方法、模型规模和任务类型影响。通过信噪比分解和跨层传播分析，揭示了量化误差的产生与累积规律，并解释了大模型对量化更鲁棒的原因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究缺乏对LLM权重量化退化机制的系统性理解，尤其不清楚为何不同条件下量化效果差异巨大，这阻碍了量化方法的选择与优化。

Method: 对不同模型家族、比特宽度（2-4位）、量化方法和下游任务进行系统实验；引入信噪比（SNR）作为度量，提出源头SNR分解（区分权重误差幅度、任务信号强度、误差与激活对齐度）和跨层传播分析（观察误差随层数衰减、保持或放大）。

Result: 4位量化通常保持性能，2位普遍退化，3位退化明显且随任务、方法和模型规模变化；误差在深层传播中可能被衰减、保持或放大，大模型表现出更弱的误差放大效应。

Conclusion: 量化退化由源头误差生成特性和跨层累积机制共同决定，SNR框架能有效解释并预测不同条件下的量化行为，为设计更优量化方案提供了理论指导。

Abstract: Post-training quantization reduces the deployment cost of large language models, yet how severely a quantized model degrades is not determined by bit-width alone. We systematically study weight-only post-training quantization across bit-widths, quantization methods, model scales and downstream tasks on multiple model families. We observe that such degradation varies substantially across these factors: 4-bit quantization usually preserves performance, 2-bit often causes broad degradation, and at 3-bit, degradation becomes apparent but varies markedly with task type, quantization method and model scale. To explain this variability, we use the signal-to-noise ratio (SNR) to measure how strongly quantization perturbs full-precision representations. We trace degradation back to two linked processes: how quantization errors arise within individual modules, and how they accumulate across layers. First, a source SNR decomposition shows that newly introduced errors depend on three factors: the magnitude of the weight error, the strength of the task-specific signal, and how strongly the quantization error aligns with task-specific activations. Different factors affect these components in distinct ways. Second, a cross-layer propagation analysis shows that these errors can be attenuated, preserved, or amplified as they pass across layers, and that larger models benefit from weaker error amplification. Together, these results establish that quantization degradation is governed by how errors are introduced at the source and how they accumulate across the network.

</details>


### [220] [A Minimal $κ$--$τ$ Logic for Risk-Sensitive Abduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08192)
*Remo Pareschi*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出一个κ-τ逻辑框架，用以在风险敏感领域中显式建模假设间交互与承诺时机的治理，支持假设合成与分析两种模式，并作为神经符号架构的符号治理层。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有溯因推理方法虽能保留多个候选解释，但未将内部、具有竞争敏感性的承诺判断与组合式跨假设交互显式结合；在风险敏感领域，过早承诺有不对称下行成本，承诺时机本身应是受治理的决策，而非仅由推理强制决定。

Method: 构建基于两个原语的κ-τ逻辑：假设间 epistemic 交互（κ）和规范性承诺阈值（τ）。假设可共存、强化或抑制，并形成涌现复合解释；承诺收缩受治理约束调控。逻辑包含合成与分析两种互补模式，共享交互关系与治理机制，分别用于向上合成原子假设或向下分解复杂状态为因果簇。

Result: 框架提供了区分“极有可能”与“值得承诺”的形式化机制，其认知参数可由神经组件（语义嵌入、生成模型）估计，规范性参数保留为显式人类治理，实现透明可审计的溯因推理。

Conclusion: κ-τ逻辑作为神经符号架构的符号治理层，适用于高 stakes 场景，通过人类治理与神经估计的分离，在表现力与可审计性之间取得平衡。

Abstract: Standard approaches to abductive reasoning can retain multiple candidate explanations, but they do not generally combine explicit compositional cross-hypothesis interaction with an internal, rival-sensitive commitment judgment. This paper argues that in risk-sensitive domains -- where premature commitment carries asymmetric downside costs -- the timing of commitment is itself a governed decision that the inferential apparatus should formally represent. We present a minimal $κ$--$τ$ logical framework built on two primitives: epistemic interaction among hypotheses ($κ$) and a normative commitment threshold ($τ$). Hypotheses may coexist, reinforce or inhibit one another, and form emergent composite explanations, while collapse into committed conclusions is regulated by governance constraints rather than forced by inference alone. The logic is developed in two complementary modes sharing the interaction relation and the governance apparatus: a synthetic mode, in which atomic hypotheses are composed upward into emergent explanations, and an analytic mode, in which complex observed states of affairs are decomposed into causal clusters of latent factors, with commitment governed at both the cluster and the factor level. The framework provides formal machinery for domains in which the distinction between highly likely and commit-worthy is operationally consequential. The $κ$--$τ$ logic is positioned as the symbolic governance layer of a neurosymbolic architecture: its epistemic parameters are naturally estimated by neural components -- semantic embeddings and generative models, as demonstrated in existing computational realizations -- while its normative parameters remain under explicit human governance, yielding transparent and auditable abductive reasoning for deployment in high-stakes settings.

</details>


### [221] [Path-dependent Discrete Amortized Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08644)
*Tiago da Silva,Esmeralda S. Whitammer,Salem Lahlou*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出一种路径相关的离散摊销推断方法，通过引入可学习的潜在动力学系统替代马尔可夫假设，以解决状态别名导致的表达能力下降和训练信号传播受阻问题，并在基准测试中取得更快收敛和更好探索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有离散摊销采样依赖确定性MDP的马尔可夫假设，导致训练时信号传播受阻，且状态别名严重降低学习采样器的表达能力，需突破该假设以提升性能。

Method: 用可学习的潜在动力学系统提升MDP，使策略依赖完整历史轨迹而非仅当前状态，并理论证明现有学习算法可扩展至该非马尔可夫设定。

Result: 在标准基准问题上，相比先前技术，方法常实现更快的学习收敛和更优的状态空间探索。

Conclusion: 路径依赖设计有效缓解马尔可夫假设的缺陷，为离散组合对象的高效摊销采样提供了更优方案。

Abstract: We consider the problem of sampling compositional and discrete objects from a given unnormalized posterior distribution. Notably, recent studies have shown that this problem can be efficiently solved by learning a deterministic Markov Decision Process (MDP) that progressively builds each object in proportion to the posterior. In this work, however, we demonstrate that the Markovian assumption can both hamper signal propagation during training and catastrophically reduce the learned sampler's expressivity due to state aliasing. To address these issues, we propose lifting the MDP with a learnable latent dynamical system that allows the underlying policy to depend on the entire past trajectory---and not only on the current state. In view of this, we refer to the resulting method as path-dependent discrete amortized inference. Importantly, we provably extend existing learning algorithms for discrete amortized samplers to our setting. In experiments on standard benchmark problems, we also show that our approach often leads to faster learning convergence and improved state space exploration relatively to prior techniques.

</details>


### [222] [LatticeMind: A Conflict-Aware Memory Primitive for Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08236)
*Heng Zhou,Lian Zhang,Yutao Fan,Tiancheng He,Siki Chen,Hejia Geng,Philip Torr,Zhenfei Yin*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出LatticeMind，一种在写入时处理矛盾的冲突感知结构化记忆，显著优于聚合基线，但在规划类任务上不如深思熟虑方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体LLM系统失败常因缺乏持久机制决定互斥主张的可信度，现有投票/辩论/裁判方法不留记录，无法追溯状态变化。

Method: 维护显式条目状态，执行廉价符号冲突检查，仅对未解决的语义冲突调用LLM调解。

Result: 在去标签的ConflictBank上准确率达0.97 vs 基线0.61（p<10^-6），去除检查器或调解器各损失12-14分；在四个规划基准中三个优于朴素合并。

Conclusion: LatticeMind有效解决事实冲突聚合，但不适用于需迭代搜索的规划任务，且结论为混合证据。

Abstract: Multi-agent LLM systems often fail not for lack of candidate answers, but because they have no persistent mechanism for deciding which incompatible claim should currently be trusted. Majority vote, debate, and judge-based selection choose an output without recording which claim wins, which is contested, or why a later update supersedes it. We present \term{LatticeMind}, a conflict-aware structured memory that handles contradiction at write time. It maintains explicit item status, applies cheap symbolic conflict checks, and invokes LLM reconciliation only for unresolved semantic cases. On a label-blind ConflictBank evaluation that removes source-name hints, LatticeMind reaches 0.97 accuracy versus 0.61 for the strongest aggregation baseline, with the gap significant at $p<10^{-6}$ by paired McNemar test. Ablations show that removing the checker or the reconciler costs 12 to 14 points. On four secondary planning benchmarks the picture is mixed: LatticeMind beats naive merge on three of four, but does not replace deliberation methods on tasks rewarding iterative search.

</details>


### [223] [Efficient Test-Time Scaling for LLM-based Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08675)
*Xuan-May Le,Minh-Tuan Tran,Ling Luo,Uwe Aickelin,Dinh Phung,Trung Le*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出SCALER框架，通过粗到细的预测方式，先用轻量级Transformer预测未来动态的粗粒度形状，再引导LLM进行固定步数的残差细化，在降低计算成本的同时提升长短期及零样本预测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的预测器虽通过测试时扩展（如迭代细化）提升精度，但计算开销大，且随着预测范围延长，容易出现全局形状不匹配的问题。

Method: SCALER采用两阶段策略：首先用轻量级Transformer预测未来序列的粗表示（形状），然后用该形状作为紧凑引导，驱动LLM进行固定步数的粗到细残差令牌细化，避免使用长描述提示和奖励模型选择，减少每步处理的令牌数。

Result: 实验表明SCALER在长短期和零样本预测任务上超过强基线，同时显著降低LLM扩展推理成本。

Conclusion: 通过显式未来形状预测和固定步细化，SCALER实现了高效且准确的时序预测，为LLM在时序任务中的实际应用提供了低成本方案。

Abstract: Long-term time series forecasting benefits from preserving global structure such as trends and seasonality. Recent LLM-based forecasters often improve accuracy through test-time scaling (e.g., iterative refinement), but these methods are computationally expensive and increasingly prone to global-shape mismatch as the prediction horizon extends. We propose SCALER, a coarse-to-fine forecasting framework that first employs a lightweight Transformer tailored to long-term shape modeling to predict a coarse representation of future dynamics. This predicted shape then serves as a compact guide for an LLM to perform test-time scaling via iterative coarse-to-fine residual token refinement, while processing substantially fewer tokens at each step. By guiding refinement with an explicit future-shape prediction, SCALER reduces reliance on long description prompts, and its fixed-step refinement avoids costly reward-model-based selection, further lowering computational overhead. Experimental results demonstrate that SCALER outperforms strong forecasting baselines in long-term, short-term and zero-shot forecasting while significantly reducing the inference cost associated with scaled LLM for time series forecasting. Code: https://github.com/xuanmay2701/SCALER.

</details>


### [224] [RippleKV: Cross-Layer KV Cache Allocation via Perturbation Propagation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08684)
*Dongjie Xu,Kai Qian,Julius,Weijie Shi,Yuxuan Sun,Minghua Tang,Fenglei Jin,Hanchi Dong,Jiajie Xu*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出 RippleKV，通过注入范数自适应扰动并测量输出 KL 散度来估计各层值缓存的敏感性，从而在给定预算下优化 KV 缓存的跨层分配，在 LongBench 上取得最佳平均性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长上下文 LLM 推理的 KV 缓存压缩方法依赖层深度、注意力统计或表征变化等间接代理指标，无法直接反映每层扰动对最终输出的影响，导致敏感层分配不足、容忍层分配过剩。

Method: RippleKV 向每层的值缓存独立注入范数自适应扰动，在小型校准集上测量模型输出端的 KL 散度，得到逐层敏感性分布；随后将敏感性分数归一化并应用指数映射生成层预算乘数，通过比例参数调节分配差异，最后归一化以保持总缓存预算不变。

Result: 在 LongBench 基准上，匹配缓存预算条件下，RippleKV 的平均性能高于所有被评估的 KV 缓存压缩方法。

Conclusion: 直接基于输出敏感性进行跨层缓存分配优于基于代理指标的方法，且该方法模型特定、不随深度单调变化，为长上下文推理的 KV 压缩提供了更有效的分配策略。

Abstract: Long-context LLM inference is bottlenecked by KV cache memory, yet distributing a limited cache budget across layers remains challenging. Existing methods rely on proxies such as layer depth, attention statistics, or representation change. These proxies do not measure how perturbations at each layer propagate to the output and may therefore cause sensitive layers to be underallocated while tolerant layers are overallocated. To address this issue, we propose RippleKV, which allocates cache across layers by estimating how perturbations to each layer's value cache affect the final predictive distribution. RippleKV independently injects norm-adaptive perturbations into each layer's value cache and measures the induced KL divergence at the model output over a small calibration set. Averaging these responses yields a sensitivity profile specific to the model that need not vary monotonically with depth. RippleKV then converts the sensitivity profile into layer budget multipliers by normalizing the sensitivity scores and applying an exponential mapping. A ratio parameter controls the allocation disparity between sensitive and tolerant layers, while a final normalization preserves the KV cache budget. Experiments on LongBench demonstrate that RippleKV achieves the highest average performance among the evaluated KV cache compression methods under matched cache budgets.

</details>


### [225] [Loss-Resilient Wireless Video Token Communication over Block Fading Channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08698)
*Bingyan Xie,Yongjeong Oh,Zihan Chen,Jihong Park,Yongpeng Wu,Wenjun Zhang*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出了WVTC框架，通过I/P令牌分级、混合打乱打包、在线调度及微调解令牌器，在无线块衰落信道下提升视频令牌传输的抗丢包重建质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频令牌传输中，块衰落会导致多个重要或相关令牌同时丢失，严重降低视频重建质量。

Method: WVTC框架：依据预测结构对I令牌赋予高优先级，用时间邻域新颖性衡量P令牌重要性；采用混合I/P令牌打乱打包以分散结构性锚点与相关时间区域；调度器基于当前块CSI，联合考虑包重要性密度、MCS解码可靠性、块容量和重要性浓度分配包；接收端用微调的detokenizer从幸存令牌重建缺失内容，无需重传。

Result: 数值结果验证了在包错误率增加时，感知质量提升且退化更平缓。
